## 0. What changed, and the ruling that reversed the fold

V409 graded Q1 AMEND and Q3 CONDITIONAL CONFIRM - Luna CONFIRM, GLM CONFIRM, Sonnet DISCREPANCY, the first two-seat CONFIRM on Q3 - and twenty conditions came back, every one of them page text. Then the operator answered the two batched questions, and his answer REVERSES this fold's headline decision.

**THE OPERATOR'S RULING.** "gapped POC" is the point of control from the **ANCHORED VOLUME PROFILE** - one of the existing POI POC lines (FOMC, Yearly, Quarterly, Monthly, Weekly and Daily POC) that price has gapped away from, NOT a session POC. The newly-closed session's H/L is also a valid dynamic TP target, revised from the static original because it is nearer. The ruling is `BUILDER_DECISION_GAPPEDPOC-PIN117_2026-10-03.md`.

**WHAT THE REVERSAL CHANGES.** The gapped-POC detector is **SOURCED** - it reads those existing lines, spliced whole at EX-34. The "UNSOURCED and NOT GRADED" declaration and **Section 16.2's five strikes are WITHDRAWN**, and the gapped-POC branch is restored to **IN SCOPE and gradeable** with its predicate re-derived against the POC lines. The predicate geometry is unchanged - the target sits AHEAD of price - because only the source was ever in dispute. The error was mine, not the seats': 16.2 rested on a premise the operator never stated, namely that a gapped POC must be a session POC.

**THE TWENTY PAGE-TEXT CONDITIONS** are answered at 17.4, one row each. The LATE clause now carries ONE polarity in both 15.4.7 and crosswalk row 6 (row 6's form governs); the crosswalk rows that pointed at absent text are fixed; the EA 10732 placement, rows 8-10's bid/Ask distinction, the declared-loss row, the alert-only scope, EA 1961/1967's fire-clear label, the set-site grep, EA 8240, the challenger row's S3 scope, the diff-class precedence, and the three overlay residues are each corrected in place. The pin-117 reading is re-checked against the operator's June 11 fact that the confirmation candle is also the retest candle and the candle on which the 5m structure bias flipped to bullish.

## Review rule, grading, and answer form

Use physical P-lines. Apply section 47 TIE-FIXED independently per question: YES plus YES confirms; YES plus DISCREPANCY conditional-confirms; DISCREPANCY plus DISCREPANCY amends; OBJECT or NO halts. A DISCREPANCY must name the packet line, the source lines it should match, and the diff that follows if the page stands.

Check any rule sentence against the spliced source on this page and against the pinned words, never against a seat summary or a symbol name. Also check the page against ITSELF in both directions: a census cell against the paragraph it summarizes, a sentence nobody edited against the corrected thing it describes, a promise against the REQUIREMENT it names, an enumerated line list against the source it names, and any paragraph against its own duplicate. Section 17.4 prints the crosswalk of all twenty V409 conditions for this round.

Verification split: builder-disk plus his-eyes only. Every hash, byte count, line count, splice check and census row was measured from disk this round. Model tier is verification-neutral: no seat can see the builder machine, so a seat verdict is a reading of this page.

## Authority and close

No source edit, build, test, tester run, key request, live action, commit or push is authorized or requested. RECON78 one-run authorization is consumed. SRJ stays alert-only and the dirty working tree is preserved. The four open defects stay separate and unresolved. Nothing here claims that any modification, revised-target deal, broker day-close close, or June 11 alert, admission or deal occurred.

P0027: ## 4 - Historical V7 Q1 proposal (superseded by section 9)
P0028:
P0029: This is a proposed design, not implemented code. The helper body and retry block do not exist in the current EA and are not claimed as source exhibits.
P0030:
P0031: **Recommendation.** Add `UjSyncBrokerTp(entryPid, target)` at the retarget site immediately after `g_mtrade.tpRef` is revised and before `tpBookedTouch` is evaluated. Resolve the live ticket from the position identifier, then require matching symbol and magic. Read and preserve that position's live SL. Submit `PositionModify(ticket, liveSL, NormalizeDouble(target,_Digits))`; a successful library return alone is insufficient: require the documented success retcode and a broker-position TP readback equal to the normalized target. If already equal, emit a named `UJTPMODIFY_SKIP` and do not count a modify attempt.
P0032:
P0033: Proposed per-instance fields: `ujTpSyncAttempts`, `ujTpSyncAttemptBarTime`, `ujTpSyncState`, and `ujTpSyncTarget`, keyed by position identifier plus trade sequence/admission bar. Attempt no more than once per evaluated M5 bar and no more than three times per target revision. Each unsuccessful request or failed exact readback consumes one attempt. After attempt three, latch terminal FAIL; unclassified retcodes must not become an unlimited retry class. Name the retryable/permanent retcode sets or explain the default. Every modify, skip, and fail event carries pair, session, sequence, admission bar, position identifier, target and attempt/state. If TP is already equal, emit `UJTPMODIFY_SKIP` and do not count a modify attempt; explicitly exclude that skip from modify-call counts. No market close on failure: original broker SL/TP remain in control, acceptance fails, and the mismatch remains visible in `UJORPHAN` until actual broker exit. Do not mark revised-price `TP_TOUCH` as a successful broker exit while sync is pending or failed; council should settle the precise managed-model state and alert/log disposition for that case.
P0034:
P0035: The retry host is after the active check at EA 11845 and before the state gate at EA 11846. EA 351 inside `MtReset` sets `g_mtrade.active=false`; the fill path sets it true at EA 10702. Model close at EA 12037 changes state to `MT_CLOSED` without clearing `active`. The proposed host sits after the active check and before the state gate, so it can service a closed-model/open-broker instance if no reset has already cleared active; verify every `MtReset` caller and the actual instance lifecycle.
P0036:
P0037: O6 admission rule: before any same-pair/same-session new setup is seeded/admitted, block it while a broker position for that pair/session remains open, including an older position. A London position does not block New York. Broker-confirmed exit ends the live instance. The proposed design should gate candidate admission immediately before the IDLE seed path at EA 8072 and recheck before EmitAlert SIGNAL at EA 10625. Current IsSessionPositionOpen(magic) filters symbol and session magic, but its call at EA 10756 follows the signal alert and the MODE_ALERT_ONLY return at EA 10740-10749; it is too late for the settled admission rule. Council must confirm the proposed sites and older same-session positions are included. This boundary controls Q3: June 11 NY admission is expected only if Q1's 5 June NY position broker-closed at its revised target beforehand; otherwise the same-session block is correct.

P0043: - Ask A: is the proposed helper, per-instance state, retry cap, exact EA insertion sites and pre-SIGNAL O6 gate complete and internally consistent for this defect? Resolve ticket by position identifier, verify symbol/magic, preserve current SL, require broker success retcode and exact normalized TP readback. Name any missing field, source site, retcode rule, MQL5 behavior, or failure-state transition. In particular, state what the model must do if revised-price TP_TOUCH occurs before broker synchronization is confirmed or after the three-attempt terminal FAIL; it must not claim a revised-price broker exit that did not occur. Verify whether the retry host can still identify the position after every `MtReset` path.
P0044: - Ask B: is the future acceptance exact and sufficient? For both June 5 NY (160.723 -> 160.298) and London (159.900 -> 159.908), require an instance-labeled retarget, accepted broker modification, exact broker TP readback, preserved SL, and actual broker TP deal exactly at the revised value before any later stop. London baseline is fully visible: 09:45 SIGNAL/deal #4 at 159.948 with SL 159.972 and TP 159.900; the 12:00 retarget precedes model TP_TOUCH at 12:10; the original broker TP fills at 12:19:21. A revised-target deal is a future predicate, not baseline evidence. Require no acceptance if modification/readback fails or the deal fills at the old target. Name identity fields in modify/skip/fail rows (pair, session, trade sequence/admission bar and position identifier); name and exclude idempotent skips from modify-attempt counts. Keep the operator-set entry open and actual deal as separate exact observations: the 5 June NY baseline signal/model entry is 160.115, Ask/deal is 160.120; do not assert they are equal or waive EXACT-PRICE-NO-LENIENCY. The current run has no modify call/event or revised-target deal; do not claim those outcomes happened.

P0047:
P0048: Current sequence on 11 June: the 14:40:22 pass evaluates the 14:35 bar. The opposite SHORT S4_ARMED holder gets `LTFFLIP` and sets `uj_saAbort` keyed to anchor, direction and bar time; the equal-tier LONG Daily-POC retest is excluded while the singleton is occupied; deferred abort is applied later. `SUPPRESSED` reports the exclusion; it does not perform it. P0049:
P0050: **Recommendation.** When `uj_saAbort` matches the current holder identity, consume that abort once before the normal IDLE seed check. Capture/retain no S4 zone, touch, entry, SL, TP, or confirmation latches. Let `GoAbort` reset the dead holder, clear the consumed deferred-abort local, then pass the current bar once through the existing IDLE seed path so the 14:35 LONG retest+confirmation is freshly evaluated. Do not rerun the whole bar or process the candidate twice. If this placement cannot safely reach the existing IDLE path in the same pass, council must give one concrete alternative state map; no S4-to-LONG state transfer is presumed safe.

Q3 outcome is conditional on that prerequisite; the baseline June 11 miss does not contain a LONG confirm, alert, admission, or deal at 160.524.

P0392: 7991:    //--- [Task 73 / Stage 3 cost side] Suppression census. DIAGNOSTIC ONLY.
P0393: 7992:    //--- Two unmeasured quantities, both needed before Stage 3 is sized:
P0394: 7993:    //---   1. The singleton discards every POI retest that arrives while a
P0395: 7994:    //---      sequence is alive. 103 candidates were ADMITTED across this
P0396: 7995:    //---      window; how many were silently dropped is unknown, and Stage 3
P0397: 7996:    //---      lengthens candidate lifetime, so it raises that number.
P0398: 7997:    //---   2. Part A carries a rule the EA does not implement - an
P0399: 7998:    //---      opposite-direction HIGHER-TIER retest replaces the candidate.
P0400: 7999:    //---      Its frequency has never been counted.
P0401: 8000:    //---
P0402: 8001:    //--- DetectPoiRetest is read-only: it fills a caller-owned struct from the
P0403: 8002:    //--- 12 POI buffers and mutates no sequence state. It is called here on the
P0404: 8003:    //--- SAME barShift the live cascade uses, so a hit is exactly a retest the
P0405: 8004:    //--- IDLE block would have consumed had the singleton been free.
P0406: 8005:    //---
P0407: 8006:    //--- Tier comparison uses g_authorityRank (lower is more authoritative),
P0408: 8007:    //--- the same ranking D-3 and G-2 already use. No distance, no size, no bar
P0409: 8008:    //--- count, no tolerance - Part A section 7 is not engaged.
P0410: 8009:    //---
P0411: 8010:    //--- Gated on InpDebugLog. Assigns nothing outside its own statics, reads
P0412: 8011:    //--- g_state / g_dir / g_anchorLine for labelling only, and cannot alter
P0413: 8012:    //--- control flow. R8 is NOT engaged.
P0414: 8013:    if(InpDebugLog && inWindow &&
P0415: 8014:       g_state > ST_IDLE && g_state != ST_ABORT && g_anchorLine >= 0)
P0416: 8015:      {
P0417: 8016:       static int s_t73_n      = 0;
P0418: 8017:       static int s_t73_higher = 0;
P0419: 8018:       static int s_t73_opp    = 0;
P0420: 8019:       static int s_t73_both   = 0;
P0421: 8020:       static int s_t73_bars   = 0;
P0422: 8021:       s_t73_bars++;
P0423: 8022:       PoiRetestResult t73_pr;

P0425: 8024:         {

P0428: 8027:          bool         t73_isOpp  = (t73_dir != g_dir);
P0429: 8028:          bool         t73_isHigh = (g_authorityRank[t73_pr.topLine] <
P0430: 8029:                                     g_authorityRank[g_anchorLine]);
P0431: 8030:          if(t73_isHigh)               s_t73_higher++;
P0432: 8031:          if(t73_isOpp)                s_t73_opp++;

P0517: ## 8 - Historical V7 review boundaries (superseded by section 9)
P0518:
P0519: Review only this complete page. Give one verdict for Q1 and Q3, answer A and B for each, cite physical P-lines, identify every remaining defect/condition, and close each question separately. Q2 is closed by both required seats and is not re-asked. Distinguish settled operator rules from implementation recommendations and future acceptance predicates. Do not claim unobserved outcomes or rely on unspliced source behavior. This review authorizes no source edit, build, tester run, live trade, Luna key, operator run word, commit, or push. The prior run authorization is consumed.
P0520: ## 9 - Historical V8 operative design and council questions (superseded by section 13)
P0521:
P0522: ### 9.1 Decision scope
P0523:
P0524: This section records the V8 proposal and question form for audit. It is superseded by section 13. Do not grade this historical answer form in place of the V9 Q1/Q3 asks. Q2 remains closed.
P0525:
P0526: ### 9.2 Q1 proposed design: position-instance manager plus exact broker TP synchronization
P0527:
P0528: **The singleton replacement is part of the defect, not a safe assumption.** Current EA lines 10641-10656 explicitly replace the one `g_mtrade` record on a later signal, and line 10656 calls `MtReset`; the same file has one `MtReset` call site plus its definition. The strategy record says London and New York are independent. Therefore v8 does not rely on per-target fields inside the replaceable singleton, nor on an argument that the retry window is practically empty.
P0529:
P0530: **Recommendation:** replace the single managed-position slot with an instance collection keyed by broker `POSITION_IDENTIFIER` plus SRJ trade sequence and admission bar. Each filled position owns its full management snapshot, entry ticket, pair, direction, session/magic, SL, current booked TP, target revision, model state, and sync state. Filling a later London/NY trade adds an instance; it never resets or overwrites another open instance. `MtReset` becomes slot-local and can retire a slot only after that PID is absent and its closing deal has been resolved. Evaluate every active instance once per closed M5 bar. This is a design requirement to preserve both positions and their retries; the packet does not claim the collection exists.
P0531:
P0532: For each instance, the retarget site updates that instance's model target, increments its target revision, resets that revision's attempt counter, emits `UJRETARGET`, and makes the immediate broker call attempt 1 before `tpBookedTouch` evaluation. The retry host runs once per evaluated M5 bar for each still-open instance, before any model-state early return, including while the model is waiting for broker exit. If the host already ran earlier on the same bar for the prior revision, the revision-keyed bar guard still permits attempt 1 for the newly retargeted value; there is at most one modify call per bar per revision and three calls total per revision. A readback-only lag is not a second call in the same bar. A successful target revision is latched; emit one `UJTPMODIFY_SKIP` when already equal and no repeated per-bar skip noise.
P0533:
P0534: The helper is execute-mode only. In `MODE_ALERT_ONLY`, or before a filled broker position resolves to the instance, it emits `UJTPMODIFY_NOPOS`/paper-only disposition, consumes no attempt, and does not claim broker synchronization. No market close is sent on modify failure. Resolve a live ticket by enumerating positions and matching PID, symbol, and expected session magic, then reselect and verify the ticket/PID before modification. Use ticket overload, never the symbol overload. Read the live SL immediately before modification; pass that exact SL (including 0.0 when there is no SL), and require normalized readback SL to equal the pre-call SL.
P0535:
P0536: `PositionModify` is a `CTrade` method. Official MQL5 documentation says its Boolean return reports basic request-structure checks and requires checking `ResultRetcode`; the ticket overload targets a ticket, while the symbol overload in hedging accounts can select the lowest-ticket position. The EA has one `CTrade g_trade` declaration, two `SetTypeFilling` calls, no `PositionModify` call, and no `SetAsyncMode` call. The build packet must explicitly verify the deployed object's async configuration and the account's netting/hedging mode before relying on readback timing or identity behavior. References: `https://www.mql5.com/en/docs/standardlibrary/tradeclasses/ctrade/ctradepositionmodify` and `https://www.mql5.com/en/docs/constants/errorswarnings/enum_trade_return_codes`.
P0537:
P0538: A modify is CONFIRMED only if the method returns true, `ResultRetcode()==TRADE_RETCODE_DONE (10009)`, the same PID/ticket remains selected, normalized TP readback equals the target revision, and normalized SL readback equals the pre-call SL. `NO_CHANGES (10025)` or an already-equal TP is `SKIP`, consumes no call, and is not counted as a modify. Retcode proposal, to be encoded as a closed table: retryable `REQUOTE (10004)`, `PRICE_CHANGED (10020)`, `TIMEOUT (10012)`, `CONNECTION (10031)`, `TOO_MANY_REQUESTS (10024)`, `MARKET_CLOSED (10018)`; immediate terminal FAIL `INVALID (10013)`, `ERROR (10011)`, `INVALID_PRICE (10015)`, `INVALID_STOPS (10016)`, `TRADE_DISABLED (10017)`, `LOCKED (10028)`, `FROZEN (10029)`. `POSITION_CLOSED (10036)` routes to broker-exit resolution. Every other retcode consumes one of the three calls and cannot retry past the cap. These are proposed classifications, not claimed facts about broker behavior; the build packet must preserve the raw code and re-verify each symbolic code against the current MQL5 table.
P0539:
P0540: Before sending a modify, compare the current executable side against the normalized target: Bid for LONG TP and Ask for SHORT TP. If price has already passed the proposed target, do not submit a stale stop level; emit terminal `UJTPMODIFY_FAIL reason=TARGET_PASSED`, keep broker SL/TP authoritative, mark acceptance failed, and retain the live instance until broker exit. This avoids spending retries on a target that can no longer meet exact-price acceptance.
P0541:
P0542: **TP_TOUCH never substitutes for the broker deal.** In MODE_EXECUTE, if the revised model target is touched while sync is PENDING, terminal FAIL, or CONFIRMED, do not set the managed instance to broker-closed and do not emit ordinary `MTEXIT`, `MTLIFE`, or `ALERT EXIT TP_TOUCH` as if execution occurred. Emit `UJTPTOUCH_UNSYNCED` (when not yet confirmed) or `UJTPTOUCH_MODEL_ONLY` (when confirmed) with PID, ticket, state, target, attempts, and live broker TP; retain the instance in `EXIT_PENDING_BROKER`, keep the retry host alive, and leave broker SL/TP in control. No market close. Emit distinct operator alert `EXIT-UNSYNCED` only once per instance/target revision; it identifies the live broker TP and cannot say the revised target filled. In MODE_ALERT_ONLY, keep the existing paper-model touch exit and clearly mark the row/alert `mode=ALERT_ONLY`; no broker fill is implied. A no-position/pending-fill row consumes no attempt.
P0543:
P0544: Resolve actual close by polling each outstanding PID in the M5 host. If the PID is gone, select its position history (`HistorySelectByPosition`) and scan deals for that PID, entry direction, closing `DEAL_ENTRY_OUT`, `DEAL_REASON_TP` or SL, deal ticket, timestamp, price, and volume. Emit `UJTPBROKEREXIT` only when the actual closing deal is identified; otherwise retain `EXIT_PENDING_BROKER`, emit `UJORPHAN`/`UJTPEXIT_PENDING`, and do not silently retire the instance. The official deal properties define `DEAL_POSITION_ID`, `DEAL_ENTRY`, `DEAL_REASON`, `DEAL_PRICE`, and `DEAL_VOLUME`; partial closes do not satisfy full-position acceptance. Reference: `https://www.mql5.com/en/docs/constants/tradingconstants/dealproperties` and `https://www.mql5.com/en/docs/trading/historyselectbyposition`.
P0545:
P0546: Every retarget/modify/skip/fail/touch/exit row is unconditional and carries: pair, session, trade sequence, admission bar, PID, current ticket, target revision, original TP, revised target, attempt/cap, sync/model state, raw method Boolean, raw retcode, readback TP and SL, pre-call SL, live volume, and reason. `UJRETARGET` must not be debug-only for acceptance. Modify-call counters exclude skip and no-position events. Synchronized rows should not repeat every bar.
P0547:
P0548: O6 is applied before candidate seed at EA 8072 and rechecked before any `LogSignal`, `A6Fired`, or `EmitAlert(SIGNAL)` side effect (currently EA 10618-10625). Both gates use the local `sess` to map the correct magic, inspect all open positions for this symbol/session, include older positions, and emit a named `UJPOSITION_BLOCKED`/`ABORT_CONCURRENCY` row. A blocked candidate emits no SIGNAL or A6Fired row and does not call `MarkSessionUsed`; London does not block NY. The second gate must run before EA 10618, not just before the alert at 10625. In alert-only mode the broker-position gate is inert, but the instance manager still prevents record replacement and maintains one-take-per-session accounting.
P0549:
P0550: **Q1 Ask A:** Is this complete and internally consistent as a design for (1) multi-instance management, (2) execute-only broker modification, (3) exact per-revision retry and retcode behavior, (4) model-touch/open-broker lifecycle, (5) broker exit proof, and (6) both pre-seed/pre-signal O6 gates? Name any remaining source site or state-transition defect. Verify the official method and retcode references cited here; do not treat a Boolean success alone as execution.
P0551:
P0552: **Q1 Ask B:** Is future acceptance exact and sufficient for both June 5 instances independently? Require unconditional instance-labeled rows; a successful modify and exact normalized TP/SL readback; unchanged SL; and a full closing broker TP deal for the same PID at the revised target exactly, with no gap/slippage leniency. A deal at the old target, SL, or any other price fails. The model touch bar need not equal the broker-deal bar. Skip events are excluded from call counts. Also require a classified diff of every admission, signal, abort, and deal after June 5 19:15 against baseline, since earlier NY broker exit can change June 8-11 behavior. Preserve the separate exact observations 160.115 model entry and 160.120 Ask/deal; the operator-set 160.524 cannot be relabeled as Ask or relaxed. One-tick stop execution 159.725 vs 159.726 is not TP tolerance.
P0553:
P0554: ### 9.3 Q3 proposed design: preserve holder order, then run the fresh candidate pipeline once
P0555:
P0556: The strategy record `.opencode/skills/srj-strategy/SKILL.md` section 117 already settles the tie: a flip-killed holder never vetoes a challenger; the same-bar flip-plus-confirm tie is unexercised and keeps current order. The 5m kill is pre-confirmation only (section 116). The June 11 baseline has `UJDEFERABORT`, `SUPPRESSED action=HELD`, RETESTBOOK hits=2, and a SHORT-only shadow CONFIRMPOLL with `confirm=0`; it has no `UJSBTELEM` row and proves neither a LONG confirm nor a June 11 outcome.
P0557:
P0558: **Do not consume the abort before the holder current-order evaluation.** Split the per-bar flow into an incumbent phase and an outer resolver. The incumbent phase preserves the current evaluation, including contender confirmation/yield and all existing early-return behavior, and returns its outcome to the resolver instead of returning from the whole per-bar function. The resolver always handles a matching pending `uj_saAbort` after the incumbent phase, including when an internal early-return path ran. The measured range EA 7457-8069 contains four returns (7578, 7580, 7601, 7621); the implementation packet must census every return in the full extracted phase and prove none can bypass resolution. The RECON78 June 11 baseline reaches the old `UJDEFERAPPLY` row at the existing EA 8462-8476 site, proving that the four earlier returns did not fire on the target pass. Keep holder evaluation and its current same-bar tie order first. If the abort still matches the holder identity, print `UJDEFERCONSUME` with old holder identity, call `GoAbort(ABORT_LTF_MISALIGN)` unless that exact holder was already reset by its existing terminal path, clear `uj_saAbort`, `uj_saA/D/T`, and invalidate stale `uj_memo_*`; preserve the diagnostic shadow/STAND-DOWN. Then invoke the extracted candidate pipeline once on the same `barShift`, `barTime`, and local `sess`: it owns the normal seed-and-candidate path from EA 8070-8429, its candidate-specific contender evaluation at EA 8430-8461, and candidate continuation from EA 8478 through the signal/admission boundary at EA 10625. It excludes only the outer incumbent deferred-abort resolver at EA 8462-8476, which the wrapper already handled. The normal IDLE path calls this same extracted unit, including its contender evaluation once. It uses normal `DetectPoiRetest`, confirmation, session/seed/1R gates, O6 checks, and signal/admission rules. It carries no S4 state, zone, latch, confirmation, or entry plan. After this one candidate-pipeline call, the outer resolver returns; it does not fall through and evaluate the fresh candidate again in the old outer tail. This is not recursive `EvaluatePerBar` and does not repeat bar-wide prepass work or incumbent evaluation.
P0559:
P0560: If the incumbent's existing contender evaluation yields to a confirmed opposite candidate before deferred-abort application, retain the existing `SIDE1C_YIELD` result and do not also consume/reseed. The pending abort no longer matches the rebound holder; emit exactly one `UJDEFERDROP reason=HOLDER_YIELDED` and clear it. This is the current-order same-bar tie behavior. If that evaluation does not yield, the matching flip-killed holder is aborted and the freed slot may run a fresh challenger pipeline on the same evaluated bar. A matching abort cannot survive into the fresh seed; a nonmatching stale abort follows the explicitly logged drop path. The release runs before O6's same-session broker-position gate; an existing open same-session position blocks the seed after the holder kill, which is the correct combined outcome.
P0561:
P0562: The incumbent candidate flow and fresh challenger flow are each evaluated once for their own identity. The prepass suppression census and read-only shadow poll run once; when a matching abort is pending, `SUPPRESSED` must not print a false `action=HELD` for the challenger that is about to receive the free slot. Candidate counters (`r2_evals`, S1F/S1G/S1C counters and any other static/bar counts) are scoped by `(barTime, phase, candidate identity)` so the incumbent and challenger are each counted once without duplicating a bar-wide total. The consume path returns after the fresh candidate pipeline, so the old outer S3/S4 and signal tail cannot evaluate it again. Preserve normal POC-over-VWAP ranking and no reseed provenance/exemption. The implementation packet must provide an exhaustive `g_mtrade` read/write migration map and counter census; no legacy singleton reference may be silently left behind.
P0563:
P0564: This design is conditional on the 14:35 LONG actually passing normal confirmation. The source must show the full `IsConfirmationCandle` implementation and the normal seed path. The 14:35 SHORT holder shadow `confirm=0` is not evidence for the LONG's normal result. Add the missing June 11 `UJSBTELEM` and normal-path confirm diagnostics to future acceptance; if the seed confirmation evaluates false, the expected outcome is no 14:40 signal and the confirmation cause is a separate defect, not something the release hook can waive.
P0565:
P0566: **Q3 Ask A:** Does the staged state map preserve (1) current holder evaluation and SIDE1C_YIELD precedence, (2) pre-confirmation 5m flip kill, (3) holder reset and fresh normal seed, (4) same-pass once-only counters and no duplicate stage evaluation, (5) shadow/STAND-DOWN, (6) O6 ordering/local session scope, and (7) POC-over-VWAP ranking? Identify any missing clear/write/return or a safer concrete map. Check against the full source excerpts in section 10.
P0567:
P0568: **Q3 Ask B:** Is this future-only acceptance gradeable? (i) Q1's same-session NY position has actual broker-close at exact 160.298 before June 11, otherwise O6 correctly blocks; (ii) source/journal prove 14:35 LONG Daily-POC retest and normal confirmation pass, with `UJSBTELEM` and normal confirm evidence; (iii) one registered LONG SIGNAL and actual deal at exactly operator-set 14:40 open 160.524, no spread tolerance and no relabeling to Ask; EA reads Ask for LONG, so if actual Ask/deal differs from 160.524 this predicate fails; (iv) no 14:45+ selection; (v) June 8 invalid SHORT remains silent for the 5m-flip reason, distinct from its baseline `S2SEEDBIAS_KILL`; (vi) 6/9 09:50 Weekly-VWAP remains refused without a reseed exemption; (vii) June 5 London 09:45 remains correct; (viii) exactly one accepted June 11 NY take for this registered pair/session/date/anchor/direction instance; (ix) the abort-consume path emits one `UJDEFERABORT`, one `UJDEFERCONSUME`, and one STAND-DOWN, with no `UJDEFERAPPLY` or false `SUPPRESSED action=HELD`; the SIDE1C_YIELD path instead records its yield and exactly one `UJDEFERDROP`, with no consume; (x) scoped counts of `UJDEFERAPPLY`, `UJDEFERDROP`, `SIDE1C_YIELD`, and `UJDEFERCONSUME` reconcile to the chosen branch; (xi) classified same-bar counter census proves no duplicate work. These are future-run predicates only. The prior run authorization is consumed.

P0570: ### 9.4 V395 finding crosswalk and reply form
P0571:
P0572: The following V395 findings are dispositions for this page, not claims that code has been changed:
P0573:
P0574: - Sonnet Q1 items 1-3 and GLM C1-C4: ADOPTED by position-instance collection, per-instance lifecycle, revision-scoped attempts, and pre-side-effect gates.
P0575: - Sonnet Q1 item 4 and GLM C6/C11/C12/S1: ADOPTED by pending-broker-exit state, distinct touch rows/alert, continued retry, and PID-matched closing-deal resolution; full TP-touch and exit source is appended.
P0576: - Sonnet Q1 items 5-7 and GLM C5/C8-C10/S2: ADOPTED by named retcode table, normalized TP/SL readback, 0-SL preservation, idempotent skip, three-call cap, and MQL docs citations. Anything beyond the closed retcode table consumes the finite cap.
P0577: - Sonnet Q1 item 8 and GLM Ask B: ADOPTED by unconditional full-identity rows, exact deal acceptance, same-session later-event diff, and explicit treatment of the Ask/open conflict and stop execution.
P0578: - GLM C7 plus Sonnet gate-side-effect finding: ADOPTED by using local session magic at both gates and placing pre-signal check before `LogSignal`/`A6Fired`; blocked candidates do not consume the session.
P0579: - Sonnet Q3 item 1 and GLM D1/D7: ADOPTED by holding the existing current-order contender evaluation before abort consumption; section 117 already rules the flip/confirm tie, so no operator question is opened.
P0580: - Sonnet Q3 items 2-4 and GLM D2-D6/D8-D9: ADOPTED by same-bar candidate pipeline extraction, full source spans, missing telemetry callout, explicit stale-state clearing, shadow preservation, once-only counters, and a named consume row.
P0581: - Sonnet Q3 item 5 and GLM O6 comments: ADOPTED by local-session gate mapping and release-before-gate ordering.
P0582: - Sonnet Q3 item 6 and GLM Q3 Ask B: ADOPTED by binding exact 160.524, naming the Ask mismatch as a fail condition, preserving no-tolerance, and requiring instance-scoped future evidence.
P0583: - GLM D10: ADOPTED by keeping one-take accounting session/day scoped; no global run-wide count is used.
P0584:
P0585: Seat answer form: quote one verdict per Q1 and Q3 (`CONFIRM`, `OBJECT`, or `DISCREPANCY`); answer A and B separately; give exact physical P-line citations; list each remaining defect/condition with its disposition; state whether Q2 remains closed; close each question. Do not infer that any proposed helper, instance collection, hook, telemetry row, broker exit, or June 11 outcome already exists. No build/run/source/key/live/commit/push authority is requested or granted.

P0809: ### Q3 incumbent region before seed locals - source lines 7457-8069
P0810:
P0811: ```mql5
P0812: 7457:          }
P0813: 7458:      }
P0814: 7459:
P0815: 7460:    //--- [Task 135 / A-3 section 5.1 / v4.2 section 3.4 errata] The
P0816: 7461:    //--- candidate-specific structural invalidation window OPENS AT BUNDLE
P0817: 7462:    //--- BINDING, not at candidate creation. Under today's architecture the
P0818: 7463:    //--- S3->S4 arming transition IS the binding point: g_zoneHi and g_zoneLo are
P0819: 7464:    //--- assigned there and nothing before it identifies a structure at all. A
P0820: 7465:    //--- candidate that has not yet adopted a zone has no candidate-specific
P0821: 7466:    //--- structure for these three flags to describe, so a 2-of-3 verdict against
P0822: 7467:    //--- it is not attributable to anything the candidate is built on.
P0823: 7468:    //---
P0824: 7469:    //--- R-Q2 keeps the flags legitimately GLOBAL - they are the panel's
P0825: 7470:    //--- current-structure flags and no per-candidate copy is wanted. Section 5.1
P0826: 7471:    //--- fixes only WHEN they may kill a candidate.
P0827: 7472:    //---
P0828: 7473:    //--- Measured, Tier 1, Task 134: of 25 aborts, 11 are freshness deaths and
P0829: 7474:    //--- SIX fired before the candidate had armed - FRESH_OPP_FVG at
P0830: 7475:    //--- S2_LTF_ALIGN 08.14 11:35, FRESH_OPP_FVG at S3_ZONE_WAIT 08.18 10:20 and
P0831: 7476:    //--- 08.18 11:50, FRESH_OB_DEAD at S2_LTF_ALIGN 08.18 16:25 and 08.18 18:05,
P0832: 7477:    //--- FRESH_OB_DEAD at S3_ZONE_WAIT 08.21 09:35. Corroborated at Tier 3 scale
P0833: 7478:    //--- by FRESHCOUNT #1050, which fires 08.03 09:25 with state=S2_LTF_ALIGN
P0834: 7479:    //--- adverse=2 verdict=ABORT against a candidate that had bound nothing.
P0835: 7480:    //---
P0836: 7481:    //--- STRICTLY RETENTION. This edit can only let a candidate live longer. It
P0837: 7482:    //--- can never kill one, and it can never admit a signal the freshness rule
P0838: 7483:    //--- would have blocked at S4 or S5, because the poll still runs there
P0839: 7484:    //--- unchanged. Same character as Stage 3a's S1WAIT / S2WAIT retention, and
P0840: 7485:    //--- deliberately the opposite character to Task 79's strictly-removal LTF
P0841: 7486:    //--- invariant, so the two journals read against each other cleanly.
P0842: 7487:    //---
P0843: 7488:    //--- SCENARIO B IS PRESERVED. The operator's 08/03 setup-1 rejection fires at
P0844: 7489:    //--- state=S5_GATE_CHECK, inside the retained range, on the same fvgDead plus
P0845: 7490:    //--- oppFvg pair. This edit does not touch it.
P0846: 7491:    //---
P0847: 7492:    //--- ACCEPTED CONSEQUENCE 1, and the reason Task 134 ran first: a candidate
P0848: 7493:    //--- freed here does not necessarily survive. It carries whatever other
P0849: 7494:    //--- pending deaths it already had, and removing the one that fires first
P0850: 7495:    //--- reveals the next (section 16.3). Expect the abort MIX to shift toward
P0851: 7496:    //--- SESSION_CLOSED, NO_TP_TARGET and LTF_MISALIGN rather than the abort
P0852: 7497:    //--- COUNT to fall.
P0853: 7498:    //---
P0854: 7499:    //--- ACCEPTED CONSEQUENCE 2, and the real risk: this is a RETENTION edit
P0855: 7500:    //--- under a SINGLETON architecture. A candidate that lives longer holds the
P0856: 7501:    //--- singleton longer and can suppress POI retests that previously seeded
P0857: 7502:    //--- their own candidates. So the candidate COUNT may FALL and SUPPRESSED may
P0858: 7503:    //--- RISE even though this edit cannot kill anything directly. Both are

P1179: 7800:    //--- seeds the replacement on the SAME bar. GoAbort sets ST_ABORT and then
P1180: 7801:    //--- calls ResetSequence, leaving g_state == ST_IDLE, which is exactly the
P1181: 7802:    //--- state the IDLE block requires. Section 3.7's no-early-return cascade is
P1182: 7803:    //--- what makes same-bar promotion possible.
P1183: 7804:    //---
P1184: 7805:    //--- Placed BEFORE the Task 73 census so a replaced retest is not ALSO
P1185: 7806:    //--- counted as suppressed - it was promoted, not discarded. That census is

P1403: 8004:    //--- IDLE block would have consumed had the singleton been free.

P1474: 8094:         //--- [P-RESQUAT-1 F-a] eviction-paired read gate: a candidate identical to
P1475: 8095:         //--- one its own abort just evicted (same line, same dir, same session,
P1476: 8096:         //--- same day) may not re-seed into the slot; the slot stays free so the
P1477: 8097:         //--- next evaluation consumes the next bar (the 57 convergence, W6b).
P1478: 8098:         //--- EXPIRE: a day-mismatched set is nonblocking and cleared here;
P1479: 8099:         //--- no timer, no bar count (R-b).
P1480: 8100:         ENUM_SRJ_DIR rsq_dir = pr.isLong ? DIR_LONG : DIR_SHORT;
P1481: 8101:         int rsq_bit = (pr.topLine >= 0 && pr.topLine < POI_NLINES) ? pr.topLine * 2 + (pr.isLong ? 0 : 1) : -1;
P1482: 8102:         bool rsq_blocked = false;
P1483: 8103:         datetime rsq_day = TC_DayStart(barTime);
P1484: 8104:         if(rsq_bit < 0)
P1485: 8105:           {
P1486: 8106:            PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA] RESEED_BLOCKED bar=%s action=INDEX-INVALID", TimeToString(iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift), TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES));
P1487: 8107:            return;
P1488: 8108:           }
P1489: 8109:         if(sess == SESSION_LONDON)
P1490: 8110:           {

P1492: 8112:            else if(rsq_bit >= 0 && (g_evictBitsLon & (1 << rsq_bit)) != 0) rsq_blocked = true;
P1493: 8113:           }

P1495: 8115:           {
P1496: 8116:            if(rsq_day != g_evictDayNY) g_evictBitsNY = 0;

P1894: 2338:                            const ENUM_SRJ_DIR dir, string &failTerm, const bool allowReclaim = false)
P1895: 2339:   {
P1896: 2340:    failTerm = "";

P1898: 2342:    double o1 = iOpen (_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift + 1);
P1899: 2343:    double c1 = iClose(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift + 1);
P1900: 2344:    double h1 = iHigh (_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift + 1);
P1901: 2345:    double l1 = iLow  (_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift + 1);
P1902: 2346:    double o0 = iOpen (_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift);
P1903: 2347:    double c0 = iClose(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift);
P1904: 2348:    if(o1 <= 0.0 || c1 <= 0.0 || o0 <= 0.0 || c0 <= 0.0)
P1905: 2349:       { failTerm = "NO_DATA"; return false; }
P1906: 2350:    double L;
P1907: 2351:    if(!ReadBuf1(g_hPoi, anchorLine, L, barShift))
P1908: 2352:       { failTerm = "NO_LINE"; return false; }
P1909: 2353:     if(L == EMPTY_VALUE || L <= 0.0)
P1910: 2354:        { failTerm = "NO_LINE"; return false; }
P1911: 2355:     //--- [P-SLDEF-1 E14] N1 counters at the VWAP/POC site. Grounding: A2
P1912: 2356:     //--- needs c1 >= L (LONG) / c1 <= L (SHORT) - "applies to VWAP and POC
P1913: 2357:     //--- alike": exact equality passes. Family by line code.
P1914: 2358:     //--- [P-SLDEF-1b E19] A2 verdict flags: set where equality is encountered,
P1915: 2359:     //--- paired at each terminal return below (no branch touched).
P1916: 2360:     bool n1_vw = false, n1_poc = false;

P1920: 2364:        if(StringFind(g_lineCode[anchorLine], "POC") >= 0) { g_n1_pocEq++; n1_poc = true; }

P1925: 2369:     if(!closeSideOk) { failTerm = "A2_CLOSE_BREAK"; if(n1_vw) g_n1_vwapInv++; if(n1_poc) g_n1_pocInv++; return false; }

P1972: ## 13 - V9 controlling contract: reproduce valid trades and manage their exits
P1973:
P1974: ### 13.1 Strategy ruling versus implementation proof
P1975:
P1976: The operator has settled the 11 June New York USDJPY setup as valid: the 14:35 bar contains the Daily-POC LONG retest and confirmation, and the required entry is the 14:40 bar open at exactly 160.524. This is not a strategy question and must not be described as unresolved. The implementation question is whether the EA releases the flip-killed SHORT holder before it suppresses the LONG, then runs the LONG once through its ordinary confirmation, admission, alert, and entry path. If that path refuses the settled setup, the refusal is an implementation defect; release must not waive ordinary confirmation or any other settled guard. The current baseline did not produce the 14:40 LONG alert/admission/deal, so it does not prove a future fill.
P1977:
P1978: ### 13.2 Closed-session target revisions are part of taking the trade correctly
P1979:
P1980: A valid entry is not correctly managed if the EA changes only its internal target. When a newly closed session high (for a LONG) or low (for a SHORT) becomes the nearer valid target under the operator's existing target rules, the EA must create a target revision for that position and synchronize the broker TP to that exact revision. Re-evaluate each eligible closed-session directional extreme while the position remains open; each strictly nearer valid target is a new revision. Do not invent a target, use a still-forming session extreme, or change nearest-target/1R rules.
P1981:
P1982: The RECON78 baseline proves the defect on two positions: 5 June London USDJPY SHORT model TP moved 159.900 to 159.908 while the broker TP stayed at 159.900 and filled there; 5 June New York USDJPY LONG model TP moved 160.723 to 160.298 and printed model TP_TOUCH while the broker TP stayed at 160.723, before the position stopped at 159.725 against SL 159.726. These are execution-model divergences, not proof that a revised broker target filled. Q1 must cover the target revision path and actual broker outcome, not just emit UJRETARGET or model TP_TOUCH.
P1983:
P1984: This day-close path is a regression, not a new rule. The earlier EU run RECON57 already fired DAY_CLOSE on a real 4 September New York EURUSD position at 23:55 and matched the prior model (BUILDER_RESULT_RECON57-DEMOGUARD-V1.md, SHA-256 1E251DDA73FEA04B2287B32EA2E0B78885C64B0318368EFA65D8FA407CD03687, lines 41-44 and 94-96; EA tree 98F6BBAC). RECON78 instead marks the 5 June New York position internally MT_CLOSED on model TP_TOUCH while leaving the broker position open; after that state change the position can stop receiving the day-close evaluation. The 5 June New York AM high did exist and closed at 19:00 per Ruling-H; this packet does not claim it was absent. The fallback branch is still required: if no eligible closed-session high/low exists, or no broker TP deal has closed the position by the daily mark, the existing universal day-close behavior must flatten every managed trade at the first 23:55 verdict-day opening price (the day-close-minus-five mark), not leave it overnight. Require the actual same-PID broker closing deal and preserve higher-priority SL/valid TP/body-break ordering. A model TP_TOUCH while the broker TP is stale or unsynchronized must not retire management state or suppress the previously working day-close path. This is the June 5 management regression; the no-eligible-session-target branch is a future acceptance condition, not a claim about the 5 June NY high.
P1985:
P1986: Historical proof carried on this page: `06_HANDOFFS/BUILDER_RESULT_RECON57-DEMOGUARD-V1.md` (2026-09-23; SHA-256 `1E251DDA73FEA04B2287B32EA2E0B78885C64B0318368EFA65D8FA407CD03687`; EA tree `98F6BBAC`). The result states:

P2029: ### 14.2 Q1 - every nearer closed-session target and restored day-close management
P2030:
P2031: Q1 verdict: CONFIRM / OBJECT / DISCREPANCY.
P2032:
P2033: The governing existing strategy rule is dynamic: while a trade is open, each newly closed session's directional extreme may become the nearer valid target (LONG=high, SHORT=low), under existing nearest-target validity. Current helper `UjClosedSessionTarget` (EA 1893-1930) is first-out-bar oriented; current call writes only `g_mtrade.tpRef` (EA 11912-11923), while current source comment imposes one revision per instance. Explain how the operative contract evaluates each eligible newly closed session and removes that one-per-trade limitation without changing entry-time validity, nearest-target ranking, or adding a new post-entry 1R floor. Never use an open-session high/low.
P2034:
P2035: The current evaluation order is documented EA 11834-11842: SL, TP_TOUCH, BREAK, HTF, DAY_CLOSE, with HTF before DAY_CLOSE. Current management gates are EA 11843-11846; model state becomes MT_CLOSED at EA 12037; only BREAK/DAY_CLOSE invoke MtCloseBrokerPosition at EA 12052-12059. Current day-close marks use g_news_dayMarks at EA 12012-12019; Friday/week-close uses the separate g_news_friMarks path. Audit these exact paths and the function's state gate. Explain why model TP_TOUCH can no longer prevent later target revisions, SL/BREAK/HTF/DAY_CLOSE evaluation, or broker-close resolution while the same PID remains open. Keep verdict bar/open reference and actual close execution distinct. Friday behavior must retain its existing separate flag semantics.
P2036:
P2037: Reviewers must return a complete transition table for: open/manage; revised TP pending; sync accepted/readback verified; retryable failure; permanent/unknown failure; target already passed on executable side; TP_TOUCH without broker deal; broker TP or SL deal; BREAK; HTF; DAY_CLOSE; market-close pending/retry; market-close terminal failure; PID/deal resolved; and position absent. For SHORT, distinguish bid-chart model touch from broker Ask-side TP execution. A model touch is MODEL_ONLY absent a same-PID closing deal. A pending or failed sync is EXIT-UNSYNCED; it never triggers a market-close fallback. A TP/SL/BREAK/HTF/DAY_CLOSE market outcome retires only after same-PID deal resolution. Failed BREAK or DAY_CLOSE close remains managed, boundedly retried and terminally visible; do not let sync failure mask other exits. Define same-bar precedence when a TP is pending and another exit qualifies, without elevating an unsynchronized model target to a fill.
P2038:
P2039: Specify full per-position identity on every event: symbol/pair, direction, session and magic, sequence/admission bar, PID, ticket, target revision/target, attempt number, raw bool and raw retcode, sync/readback result, pre-call SL and post-call SL, close state, deal ticket, deal PID, entry/exit type, volume, and reason. Require ticket lookup by PID, nonzero ticket, PositionSelectByTicket, matching symbol/session magic/PID, and one stable managed record per broker PID; a second London/NY trade cannot overwrite an open instance. Pin hedging/netting policy (hard-refuse netting if independent simultaneous instances cannot be represented), CTrade sync/async mode, and ticket overload. Preserve existing SL including no-SL state.
P2040:
P2041: The target modify contract is explicit: maximum three actual modify calls per target revision; at most one per evaluated M5 bar per revision. Capture boolean and ResultRetcode immediately after each call. Give exhaustive retryable and permanent retcode sets, including the source/API-grounded meaning of NO_CHANGES, INVALID_STOPS, PRICE_CHANGED, TIMEOUT, CONNECTION, POSITION_CLOSED and TARGET_PASSED. NO_CHANGES is a skip only after exact normalized TP readback already equals this revision; the skip is not a modify call and does not spend the attempt budget. POSITION_CLOSED routes to same-PID history/deal resolution, never retry against a replacement PID. Any other nonaccepted result/readback mismatch consumes a capped attempt; unknown codes fail visibly and do not retry indefinitely. After the cap, leave original broker protection intact, continue managing other exit legs, label terminal sync failure, and do not claim target acceptance. Normalize only to symbol tick/price rules; exact target remains required. If executable-side market has already passed target, use an explicit TARGET_PASSED guard and defined precedence/state; never issue an invalid past-target modification or silently reinterpret it as a fill.

(5) One symbol named both the forming bar and the general-form retest bar. (6) The June 8 preservation control was missing from the acceptance. P2081: ### 15.1 Governing operator pins, spliced verbatim
P2082:
P2083: ```mql5
P2084: 28: - UNIVERSAL day-close rule (his clarification 2026-09-21, verbatim: "the experiment is about the trend following setup when the HTF flip to the other direction, close or hold. the decisive exit rule is always exit upon near the day close. the rule is 5 minutes before candle close, which has been recorded on the previous session"). Amended point: the experiment tests flip-response (close-or-hold) on trend-following setups when HTF flips the other direction; the DECISIVE exit rule is ALWAYS exit near the day close, 5 minutes before the candle close, every managed trade regardless of regime. Later word amends earlier scope: the F3 day-close leg is universal session discipline, not mean-reversion-scoped; no trade holds overnight by design. EXECUTION PINNED 2026-09-25 (his chart ruling): the DAY_CLOSE leg executes at the 23:55 opening price on the verdict day (Friday included), never at the next-day open; purpose avoid swap fee + new-day widened spread (his chart: 7 Sep 00:00 spread 18); the v225-era weekend-gap-nextOpen semantic is retired for DAY_CLOSE by this later word; BREAK-leg next-open untouched.

P2088: 32: - MANAGE-NEAREST (his words 2026-09-26 + EXITMODEL-1 Q6 documented: "yes, the nearest because price is dynamic so which ever valid TP target is the nearest, even if less than 1R after the entry and revision"): REJECT sub-1R setups at entry AND always exit on the nearest valid target in management - entry-time TP (must clear 1R) vs dynamic revised TP (newer session close H/L or gapped POC, exited even when revised below 1R). P2090:

SYMMETRY-NEAREST (his words 2026-10-02, verbatim: "once a session is closed, a floating trade can retarget that new session high or low as a valid tp target as the nearest"): the retarget object is the closed-session extreme in the trade's direction (LONG takes the high, SHORT takes the low), always as the nearest valid target.
P2093: ```
P2094:
P2095: ```mql5
P2096: 34: - EXACT-PRICE-NO-LENIENCY (his words 2026-10-02: entry at the next-candle open matched for the ideal entry, faster than his reaction time; "i want the exact tp target as the exit"; "my logic is previse [his spelling] and has no wiggle room"): entry fills at the opening price with zero leniency; exits fill at the exact booked/revised target with zero leniency. Spread drift and evaluation-lag deviations are diagnosed as defects, never granted as tolerance. His question back ("you mean like the spread drift?") names the only concern his rule admits: the automation must be fast enough to match the open.

P2098:
P2099: ```mql5
P2100: 54: - POST-ENTRY-CLOSED (his correction 2026-09-26, third wrong-bar instance: UJ3 litigated the 14:45 body after a 14:35-confirm/14:40-open execution across v287-v290). Amended point: selection ENDS at entry - retest bar N + confirmation bar N + entry N+1 open, then the book is closed to selection evidence. No post-entry bar is ever graded, cited, or diagnosed as a selection pass (confirm-once universal: later bars never re-litigate, ledger 750). The 14:45 CONFIRMPOLL confirm=0 row is post-entry territory (line-identity fact only); the decision-pass row (14:40 pass, CONFIRMPOLL bar=14:35 confirm=1) is the whole selection evidence. Builder defect owned: confirm-once was on file since ledger 750 and unconsulted - record-first failure, withdrawn across packet/relay/result/ledger the same turn. P2101: ```
P2102:
P2103: ```mql5
P2104: 86: - PRIOR-CLOSE-IRRELEVANT (his words 2026-09-25, verbatim: "the prior candlestick interaction with the D POC at 14:30 it is a break below but the next candle open of 14:35 is higher making it a valid retest. the 14:30 candle close is not relevant and not accounted."). Amended point: the bar before the retest never judges; judge the retest bar's open side plus the next close holding (no close back over to invalidate or break). Break-then-reclaim IS the setup (sweep-then-retest family). 6/11 USDJPY 14:35 instance (retest-plus-confirmation same bar per the banked 2026-09-23 SAME-CANDLE rule). Council route: the EA checks the prior close (EA 2224-2225) - wrong bar.

P2106:
P2107: ```mql5
P2108: 105: - TOUCH-OR-BREAK (his confirmation rule, verbatim: "keep it either valid retest rouch or break with a candle body close", typos his). Amended point: a confirmation is EITHER (a) a valid retest TOUCH of the line, OR (b) a BREAK with a candle BODY CLOSE through it. Zero margin, zero tolerance ("no extra margin", "i do not have buffer tolerace", typos his - extends his NO-TOLERANCE principle into confirmation geometry). The A2/BODY predicate machinery must implement exactly this touch-or-break shape; margin bands, tolerance bands, and extra-point thresholds are forbidden rules, never tunables. His Q2 answer "which line?" is answered: the Daily-POC line at 160.523 (alert rows name [D-POC +1] at 160.523, 3x same window).
P2109: ```
P2110:
P2111: ```mql5
P2112: 107: - SAME-CANDLE-PERMITTED (his clarification, verbatim: "the candle that made the valid retest can also be the confirmation candle, closing into the direction of the setup bias. that does not mean it must do that, each step can be a seperate candle that made the retest such as the 9:35 candle and the the cofirmation candle at 9:40 to then closing and then entry at the 9:45 open candle entry", typos his). Amended point: same-bar retest-plus-confirmation is PERMITTED when the bar closes into setup-bias direction, never REQUIRED; the general form is separate candles (09:35 retest bar + 09:40 confirmation bar + 09:45 open entry). Builder's conflation WITHDRAWN (the relay/result takes-sheet treated the 09:40-pass poll as the confirmation read).
P2113: ```
P2114:
P2115: ```mql5
P2116: 112: - CONFIRMATION-CANONICAL (his TOUCH-OR-BREAK rule, verbatim: "keep it either valid retest rouch or break with a candle body close", typos his). A confirmation is EITHER (a) a valid retest TOUCH of the line, OR (b) a BREAK with a candle BODY CLOSE through it. Zero margin, zero tolerance, no buffer bands, no extra points - margins are forbidden rules, never tunables. Forms: same-bar permitted (retest bar confirms iff it closes into setup-bias direction); general form separate bars (retest bar, then confirmation bar, then next-open entry). A retest-bar poll is NEVER cited as a confirmation read (BAR-ROLE). This pin amends all prior margin/term language anywhere it survives: where an old line contradicts it, this line governs (later words amend earlier).
P2117: ```
P2118:
P2119: ```mql5
P2120: 114: - BAR-MAPPING-CANONICAL (his TIMING words + EA convention): confirmation bar evaluated at the next pass (09:40 bar at 09:45 pass; 14:35 bar at 14:40:22 pass); retest-bar polls never substitute. A missing confirmation-bar poll (dead seed) is chart-only evidence, never a machine inference.
P2121: ```
P2122:
P2123: ```mql5
P2124: 117: - FLIP-KILLED-NEVER-VETOES (his words 2026-10-02, answering whether a held trade whose 5m bias turns against it still blocks a new trade: "like the 5m bias? if so then no"): a flip-killed holder never vetoes a challenger - the new trade does not wait. Narrow reading recorded: this rules the flip case; the flip-plus-confirm same-bar tie stays unexercised and keeps current order. LIVE-TRADE-BLOCKS-ALL (his words 2026-10-02: "do not forget the rule of only one entry per session per pair. if i already inside a trade, i no longer consider a new setup"): a live position of the session blocks every new setup - the veto-release rule above governs candidate-vs-candidate contention only (no live position); it never re-opens a session that already holds his trade.
P2125: ```
P2126:
P2127: SKILL line 32 is now spliced, and that closes the gapped-POC question on the evidence rather than by assertion: it names a newer session close or a gapped POC as the dynamic revised target. v15 asserted the source was in scope without carrying the pin that scopes it; both seats were right to refuse it.
P2128:
P2129: Applied: June 11 New York USDJPY Daily-POC LONG is settled valid - 14:35 retest plus confirmation on one bar, entry at the 14:40 open exactly 160.524, 14:45 or later never grading selection. The prior-close pin removes the prior bar's judgment AND requires the retest bar's open side to be judged plus the next close holding. The exact-price pin fixes the reference and names spread drift a diagnosed defect. The flip-kill pin decides the branch. No seat asks the operator anything and none is asked.
P2130:
P2131: Carried: V396 (a)-(i) at 13.3; V397 at 14.4; the V398 to V403 sets in their grades; the V404 dispositions in section 8 of that grade.

P2445: 8782:          //--- is still truncated.

P2448: 8785:          //--- cross-tabulation needs no arithmetic downstream. Task 126 reported the
P2449: 8786:          //--- LEGACYONLY cell as zero when its own data showed otherwise; a token

P2453: 8790:          //---               XOB's own promotion, which section 5.7 excludes

P2517: 8854:       //--- [Task 133 / A-3 section 5.7 / operator ruling] LIVE promotion-bounded
P2518: 8855:       //--- in-play test. ADMISSION-CHANGING, and the only admission-changing edit
P2519: 8856:       //--- in this task.
P2520: 8857:       //---
P2521: 8858:       //--- Operator ruling, verbatim: an order block that becomes a promoted XOB on
P2522: 8859:       //--- the same candle you would enter on is NOT tradeable, because "price has
P2523: 8860:       //--- not retraced to the XOB yet, it was only retracting to the ordinary OB
P2524: 8861:       //--- before it was promoted to XOB." A witness that predates promotion is
P2525: 8862:       //--- therefore not a witness. On the operator's own 08/17 setup the ordering
P2526: 8863:       //--- was promotion 05:30, witness swing 09:10, confirmation 15:50.
P2527: 8864:       //---
P2528: 8865:       //--- Measured at Tier 1 (Tasks 126, 127): 5 of the 11 live in-play verdicts
P2529: 8866:       //--- rest on a swing that predates the zone's own promotion, and every one is
P2530: 8867:       //--- a zone promoted 0-10 minutes before the evaluation bar with swings=0.
P2531: 8868:       //--- 21 further bars are in play under this bound and read out of play today.
P2532: 8869:       //---
P2533: 8870:       //--- Threshold-free. The loop limit is Bars(), a structural quantity, and the
P2534: 8871:       //--- only substantive terminator is the promotion-time comparison. Tasks 126
P2535: 8872:       //--- and 127 measured the 500-slot cap as non-binding at Tier 1 - capped and
P2536: 8873:       //--- uncapped verdicts agreed on all 56 bars - so no cap is carried here.
P2537: 8874:       //---
P2538: 8875:       //--- SCOPE. XOB-sourced zones only, per section 7.27: buffer 33 is written
P2539: 8876:       //--- from the same object pointer as buffers 22/23/31 and describes the XOB
P2540: 8877:       //--- zone alone. An FVG has no promotion time of its own. haveFvg is false on
P2541: 8878:       //--- every bar today (EA-120 limb 2), so the FVG branch has no live instance.
P2542: 8879:       //--- ZoneInPlay and ZoneAdoptable are deliberately NOT changed here: the
P2543: 8880:       //--- former is reachable only through the haveFvg downgrade and the latter
P2544: 8881:       //--- governs replacement at S4, not admission at S3. Logged as EA-132.
P2545: 8882:       //---
P2546: 8883:       //--- FAIL CLOSED on an unreadable promotion time. Without it, promotion
P2547: 8884:       //--- cannot be shown to precede the witness, which is what the ruling
P2548: 8885:       //--- requires. Zero Tier 1 instances with a populated zone, so this path is
P2549: 8886:       //--- inert today; the census counts it so the population becomes known.
P2550: 8887:       //---
P2551: 8888:       //--- ? PLACEMENT IS LOAD-BEARING. ? This block sits AFTER the S3INPLAY print
P2552: 8889:       //--- and AFTER the Task 126 and Task 127 shadow blocks have already read
P2553: 8890:       //--- s31_inPlay, and BEFORE the arming condition. Consequence, deliberate:
P2554: 8891:       //--- S3INPLAY, and the legacy= / legacyVia= fields of XOBINPLAY and
P2555: 8892:       //--- XOBINPLAY2, all keep reporting the UNCHANGED legacy ladder verdict. The
P2556: 8893:       //--- committed verdict is reported only on the new INPLAYCOMMIT line. That is
P2557: 8894:       //--- what makes unc_wide= on XOBINPLAY2 an INDEPENDENT oracle for committed=
P2558: 8895:       //--- here: two separate implementations of the same walk, in one log, on the
P2559: 8896:       //--- same bar. They must agree on every bar where applied=1 and bounded=1.
P2560: 8897:       //---
P2561: 8898:       //--- s31_via is deliberately NOT overwritten. Nothing downstream reads it,
P2562: 8899:       //--- and leaving it alone keeps the legacy ladder's verdict legible.
P2563: 8900:       bool     t133_applied = false;

P2618: 8955:

P4413: 10857: //====================== [P-NEWS-1 E20/E21/E22] news-blackout census =================
P4414: 10858: //--- Print-only. No verdict moves, no selection changes, MTEXIT untouched.
P4415: 10859: //--- E20 pinned table: 11 {eventTimeET, kind} rows transcribed from
P4416: 10860: //--- DRAFT_NEWS-EVENTS-2026.csv @ SHA256 5FFF5C76...EF1F134. No broker-time
P4417: 10861: //--- field, no stored offset: ET wall -> server at read time through
P4418: 10862: //--- TC_ZoneToServer(..., TZ_NEWYORK), the same converter the session
P4419: 10863: //--- windows use (US + server DST rules inside TickCore, evaluated on the
P4420: 10864: //--- naive wall timestamp; event hours 08:30/14:00 ET never touch the
P4421: 10865: //--- 02:00 transition hours, so the documented boundary-hour bound cannot
P4422: 10866: //--- engage these rows). Accessors are switch-based: no init order, no
P4423: 10867: //--- global-constructor dependence.
P4424: 10868: //--- E21: SrjInNewsBlackout is THE predicate body. The census calls it now;
P4425: 10869: //--- the exit side and the entry side will call the same body later. There
P4426: 10870: //--- is no second implementation (E21 halt-1 satisfied by construction).
P4427: 10871: //--- Window [newsBarOpen - 1*PS, newsBarOpen + 2*PS); newsBar containment
P4428: 10872: //--- [barOpen, barOpen + PS). No literal minutes: the shape is PS multiples;
P4429: 10873: //--- the /60 in offsetMinutes is a seconds-to-minutes unit, not a window term.
P4430: 10874: #define SRJ_NEWS_ROWS 11
P4431: 10875: #define SHADOW_NEWS true
P4432: 10876: //--- Pilot range (server frame) for inWindow membership. From RECON1_P1 per
P4433: 10877: //--- the packet's "full window" term — NOT derived from Bars()/iTime: those
P4434: 10878: //--- return the full broker history depth (years), and iTime(0) at lazy-init
P4435: 10879: //--- is the first bar, so data-derived bounds exclude every later window
P4436: 10880: //--- (RECON12 measured rowsInWindow=0 on exactly this defect). If the packet
P4437: 10881: //--- ever moves window, these two lines move with it.
P4438: 10882: #define SRJ_PILOT_FROM D'2026.08.26 00:00'
P4439: 10883: #define SRJ_PILOT_TO   D'2026.09.10 00:00'
P4440: 10884: string SrjNewsET(const int i)
P4441: 10885:    {
P4442: 10886:     switch(i)
P4443: 10887:       {
P4444: 10888:        case  0: return "2026-09-04 08:30";
P4445: 10889:        case  1: return "2026-09-11 08:30";
P4446: 10890:        case  2: return "2026-09-16 14:00";
P4447: 10891:        case  3: return "2026-10-02 08:30";
P4448: 10892:        case  4: return "2026-10-14 08:30";
P4449: 10893:        case  5: return "2026-10-28 14:00";
P4450: 10894:        case  6: return "2026-11-06 08:30";
P4451: 10895:        case  7: return "2026-11-10 08:30";
P4452: 10896:        case  8: return "2026-12-04 08:30";
P4453: 10897:        case  9: return "2026-12-09 14:00";
P4454: 10898:        case 10: return "2026-12-10 08:30";
P4455: 10899:       }
P4456: 10900:     return "";
P4457: 10901:    }
P4458: 10902: string SrjNewsKind(const int i)
P4459: 10903:    {
P4460: 10904:     switch(i)
P4461: 10905:       {
P4462: 10906:        case  0: return "NFP";
