P4471: 10915:        case  9: return "FOMC";
P4472: 10916:        case 10: return "CPI";
P4473: 10917:       }
P4474: 10918:     return "";
P4475: 10919:    }
P4476: 10920: //--- "YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM" -> naive wall datetime. StringToTime is server-frame
P4477: 10921: //--- so the pinned format is parsed manually; the result is NEVER passed to
P4478: 10922: //--- iTime/CopyBuffer without TC_ZoneToServer first.
P4479: 10923: datetime SrjNewsEtWall(const int i)
P4480: 10924:    {
P4481: 10925:     string s = SrjNewsET(i);
P4482: 10926:     int y  = (int)StringSubstr(s, 0, 4);
P4483: 10927:     int mo = (int)StringSubstr(s, 5, 2);
P4484: 10928:     int d  = (int)StringSubstr(s, 8, 2);
P4485: 10929:     int h  = (int)StringSubstr(s, 11, 2);
P4486: 10930:     int mi = (int)StringSubstr(s, 14, 2);
P4487: 10931:     return TC_MakeTime(y, mo, d, h, mi);
P4488: 10932:    }
P4489: 10933: datetime g_news_newsBar[SRJ_NEWS_ROWS];
P4490: 10934: datetime g_news_winS[SRJ_NEWS_ROWS];
P4491: 10935: datetime g_news_winE[SRJ_NEWS_ROWS];
P4492: 10936: int      g_news_offMin[SRJ_NEWS_ROWS];
P4493: 10937: int      g_news_inWin[SRJ_NEWS_ROWS];
P4494: 10938: int      g_news_halted[SRJ_NEWS_ROWS];
P4495: 10939: int      g_news_rowSnap[SRJ_NEWS_ROWS];
P4496: 10940: int      g_news_nWin = 0;
P4497: 10941: int      g_news_overlaps = 0;
P4498: 10942: string   g_news_overlapList = "";
P4499: 10943: bool     g_news_init = false;
P4500: 10944: int      g_news_memberBars = 0;
P4501: 10945: int      g_news_slimbInWin = 0;
P4502: 10946: int      g_news_s5InWin = 0;
P4503: 10947: int      g_news_flatNews = 0;
P4504: 10948: int      g_news_flatDay = 0;
P4505: 10949: int      g_news_flatWeek = 0;
P4506: 10950: datetime g_news_dayMarks[32];
P4507: 10951: int      g_news_dayDone[32];
P4508: 10952: int      g_news_dayN = 0;
P4509: 10953: string   g_news_friET = "";
P4510: 10954: datetime g_news_friMarks[8];
P4511: 10955: int      g_news_friDone[8];
P4512: 10956: int      g_news_friN = 0;
P4513: 10957: bool SrjInNewsBlackout(const datetime barOpen, string &kindOut, int &posOut, int &rowOut)
P4514: 10958:    {
P4515: 10959:     kindOut = "-"; posOut = -1; rowOut = -1;
P4516: 10960:     if(!g_news_init) return false;
P4517: 10961:     long ps = (long)PeriodSeconds(PERIOD_CURRENT);
P4518: 10962:     for(int i = 0; i < SRJ_NEWS_ROWS; i++)
P4519: 10963:       {
P4520: 10964:        if(g_news_inWin[i] == 0 || g_news_halted[i] == 1) continue;
P4521: 10965:        if(barOpen < g_news_winS[i] || barOpen >= g_news_winE[i]) continue;
P4522: 10966:        kindOut = SrjNewsKind(i); rowOut = i;
P4523: 10967:        if(barOpen < g_news_newsBar[i]) posOut = 0;
P4524: 10968:        else if(barOpen < g_news_newsBar[i] + ps) posOut = 1;
P4525: 10969:        else posOut = 2;
P4526: 10970:        return true;
P4527: 10971:       }
P4528: 10972:     return false;
P4529: 10973:    }
P4530: 10974: //--- Lazy init on the first closed bar. Conversions need no history; the E21
P4531: 10975: //--- gap check does NOT run here (tester series only extend to the current
P4532: 10976: //--- bar — September bars don't exist yet on the first August bar, and a
P4533: 10977: //--- check now would false-halt; RECON12b measured exactly that). Pilot
P4534: 10978: //--- range is SRJ_PILOT_FROM/TO.
P4535: 10979: void SrjNewsInit()
P4536: 10980:    {
P4537: 10981:     if(g_news_init) return;
P4538: 10982:     g_news_init = true;
P4539: 10983:     long ps = (long)PeriodSeconds(PERIOD_CURRENT);
P4540: 10984:     for(int i = 0; i < SRJ_NEWS_ROWS; i++)
P4541: 10985:       {
P4542: 10986:        datetime wall = SrjNewsEtWall(i);
P4543: 10987:        datetime srv  = TC_ZoneToServer(wall, TZ_NEWYORK);
P4544: 10988:        g_news_offMin[i] = (int)(((long)srv - (long)wall) / 60);
P4545: 10989:        datetime nb = (datetime)(((long)srv / ps) * ps);
P4546: 10990:        g_news_newsBar[i] = nb;
P4547: 10991:        g_news_winS[i] = (datetime)((long)nb - ps);
P4548: 10992:        g_news_winE[i] = (datetime)((long)nb + 2 * ps);
P4549: 10993:        g_news_inWin[i] = (g_news_winE[i] > SRJ_PILOT_FROM && g_news_winS[i] < SRJ_PILOT_TO) ? 1 : 0;
P4550: 10994:        g_news_halted[i] = 0;
P4551: 10995:        g_news_rowSnap[i] = 0;
P4552: 10996:       }
P4553: 10997:     //--- Day marks: 16:55 ET (= 17:00 daily close minus 5 min, the dayFlat
P4554: 10998:     //--- census definition, quoted in BLACKOUT_CENSUS) per calendar date in
P4555: 10999:     //--- range; Friday marks: 17:00 ET (the weekFlat census definition). Both
P4556: 11000:     //--- resolved through the same converter. Noon-dow is zone-safe: at
P4557: 11001:     //--- midday the ET and server dates always agree.
P4558: 11002:     g_news_dayN = 0;
P4559: 11003:     g_news_friN = 0;
P4560: 11004:     g_news_friET = "";
P4561: 11005:     datetime cur = TC_DayStart(SRJ_PILOT_FROM);
P4562: 11006:     while(cur < SRJ_PILOT_TO && g_news_dayN < 32)
P4563: 11007:       {
P4564: 11008:        MqlDateTime dd; TimeToStruct(cur, dd);
P4565: 11009:        g_news_dayMarks[g_news_dayN] = TC_ZoneToServer(TC_MakeTime(dd.year, dd.mon, dd.day, 16, 55), TZ_NEWYORK);
P4566: 11010:        g_news_dayDone[g_news_dayN] = 0;
P4567: 11011:        g_news_dayN++;
P4568: 11012:        MqlDateTime noon; TimeToStruct(TC_MakeTime(dd.year, dd.mon, dd.day, 12, 0), noon);
P4569: 11013:        if(noon.day_of_week == 5 && g_news_friN < 8)
P4570: 11014:          {
P4571: 11015:           g_news_friMarks[g_news_friN] = TC_ZoneToServer(TC_MakeTime(dd.year, dd.mon, dd.day, 17, 0), TZ_NEWYORK);
P4572: 11016:           g_news_friDone[g_news_friN] = 0;
P4573: 11017:           g_news_friN++;
P4574: 11018:           string one = StringFormat("%04d-%02d-%02d 17:00", dd.year, dd.mon, dd.day);
P4575: 11019:           g_news_friET += ((g_news_friET == "") ? "" : "|") + one;
P4576: 11020:          }
P4577: 11021:        cur = TC_ShiftDayStart(cur, 1);
P4578: 11022:       }
P4579: 11023:    }
P4580: 11024: //--- "Open" for the flat census. g_mtrade.active is STICKY: it is set at
P4581: 11025: //--- fill and cleared only by the next fill's MtReset, so a closed trade
P4582: 11026: //--- still reads active. state goes MT_CLOSED at close (normal path). Both
P4583: 11027: //--- conditions together are the true open interval (RECON12b overcounted
P4584: 11028: //--- dayFlat 12 and weekFlat 2 on the sticky flag alone).
P4585: 11029: bool SrjNewsIsOpen()
P4586: 11030:    {
P4587: 11031:     return (g_mtrade.active && g_mtrade.state != MT_CLOSED);
P4588: 11032:    }
P4589: 11033: //--- End-of-run row finalization. Full history exists now, so the E21 gap
P4590: 11034: //--- check is sound: the event instant must sit inside a real bar (exact
P4591: 11035: //--- containment, never snapped). Halted rows print HALT plus their ROW
P4592: 11036: //--- with inWindow=0 and are excluded from every expectation.
P4593: 11037: void SrjNewsFinalize()
P4594: 11038:    {
P4595: 11039:     long ps = (long)PeriodSeconds(PERIOD_CURRENT);
P4596: 11040:     g_news_nWin = 0;
P4597: 11041:     for(int i = 0; i < SRJ_NEWS_ROWS; i++)
P4598: 11042:       {
P4599: 11043:        if(g_news_inWin[i] == 1)
P4600: 11044:          {
P4601: 11045:           datetime srv = TC_ZoneToServer(SrjNewsEtWall(i), TZ_NEWYORK);
P4602: 11046:           if(iBarShift(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, srv, true) < 0)
P4603: 11047:             {
P4604: 11048:              g_news_halted[i] = 1; g_news_inWin[i] = 0;
P4605: 11049:              string haltLine = StringFormat("[SRJ-EA] BLACKOUT_HALT_ROW kind=%s eventTimeET=%s reason=GAP_NO_BAR",
P4606: 11050:                                             SrjNewsKind(i), SrjNewsET(i));
P4607: 11051:              LwAudit("BLACKOUT_HALT", haltLine);
P4608: 11052:              Print(haltLine);
P4609: 11053:             }
P4610: 11054:           else
P4611: 11055:              g_news_nWin++;
P4612: 11056:          }
P4613: 11057:        int spanned = (int)(((long)g_news_winE[i] - (long)g_news_winS[i]) / ps);
P4614: 11058:        string rl = StringFormat("[SRJ-EA] BLACKOUT_ROW kind=%s eventTimeET=%s newsBarOpen=%s windowStart=%s windowEnd=%s offsetMinutes=%d inWindow=%d barsSpanned=%d",
P4615: 11059:                                 SrjNewsKind(i), SrjNewsET(i),
P4616: 11060:                                 TimeToString(g_news_newsBar[i], TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES),
P4617: 11061:                                 TimeToString(g_news_winS[i], TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES),
P4618: 11062:                                 TimeToString(g_news_winE[i], TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES),
P4619: 11063:                                 g_news_offMin[i], g_news_inWin[i], spanned);
P4620: 11064:        LwAudit("BLACKOUT_ROW", rl);
P4621: 11065:        Print(rl);
P4622: 11066:       }
P4623: 11067:     for(int a = 0; a < SRJ_NEWS_ROWS; a++)
P4624: 11068:         for(int b = a + 1; b < SRJ_NEWS_ROWS; b++)
P4625: 11069:            if(g_news_inWin[a] == 1 && g_news_inWin[b] == 1 &&
P4626: 11070:               g_news_winS[a] < g_news_winE[b] && g_news_winS[b] < g_news_winE[a])
P4627: 11071:              { g_news_overlaps++; g_news_overlapList += ((g_news_overlaps > 1) ? "|" : "") + SrjNewsKind(a) + "@" + SrjNewsET(a) + "x" + SrjNewsKind(b) + "@" + SrjNewsET(b); }
P4628: 11072:    }
P4629: 11073: //--- Per-closed-bar census hook. Read-only: counts and prints only. Called at
P4630: 11074: //--- the END of OnTick so g_mtrade reflects the settled post-eval state.
P4631: 11075: void SrjNewsOnBar(const datetime barTime)
P4632: 11076:    {
P4633: 11077:     string k; int pos, row;
P4634: 11078:     if(SrjInNewsBlackout(barTime, k, pos, row))
P4635: 11079:       {
P4636: 11080:        g_news_memberBars++;
P4637: 11081:        string bl = StringFormat("[SRJ-EA] BLACKOUT_BAR barTime=%s kind=%s pos=%s",
P4638: 11082:                                 TimeToString(barTime, TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES), k,
P4639: 11083:                                 (pos == 0) ? "PRE" : ((pos == 1) ? "NEWS" : "POST"));
P4640: 11084:        LwAudit("BLACKOUT_BAR", bl);
P4641: 11085:        Print(bl);
P4642: 11086:       }
P4643: 11087:     //--- Flat populations, edge-triggered, once each. "Open" = SrjNewsIsOpen().
P4644: 11088:     for(int i = 0; i < SRJ_NEWS_ROWS; i++)
P4645: 11089:       {
P4646: 11090:        if(g_news_inWin[i] == 0 || g_news_rowSnap[i] == 1) continue;
P4647: 11091:        if(barTime >= g_news_winS[i])
P4648: 11092:          { g_news_rowSnap[i] = 1; if(SrjNewsIsOpen()) g_news_flatNews++; }
P4649: 11093:       }
P4650: 11094:     for(int d = 0; d < g_news_dayN; d++)
P4651: 11095:       {
P4652: 11096:        if(g_news_dayDone[d] == 1) continue;
P4653: 11097:        if(barTime >= g_news_dayMarks[d])
P4654: 11098:          { g_news_dayDone[d] = 1; if(SrjNewsIsOpen()) g_news_flatDay++; }
P4655: 11099:       }
P4656: 11100:     for(int f = 0; f < g_news_friN; f++)
P4657: 11101:       {
P4658: 11102:        if(g_news_friDone[f] == 1) continue;
P4659: 11103:        if(barTime >= g_news_friMarks[f])
P4660: 11104:          { g_news_friDone[f] = 1; if(SrjNewsIsOpen()) g_news_flatWeek++; }
P4661: 11105:       }
P4662: 11106:    }
P4663: 11107:
P4664: 11108: //====================== OnInit =========================================
P4665: 11109: int OnInit()
P4666: 11110:   {
P4667: 11111:    InitAuthorityTable();
P4668: 11112:
P4669: 11113:    if(!(bool)MQLInfoInteger(MQL_TESTER)) TC_DetectServerOffset();
P4670: 11114:    TC_DetectServerOffsetNow();
P4671: 11115:
P4672: 11116:    {
P4673: 11117:     datetime srvNow   = TimeCurrent();
P4674: 11118:     datetime gmtNow   = TimeGMT();
P4675: 11119:     bool     inTester = (bool)MQLInfoInteger(MQL_TESTER);
P4676: 11120:
P4677: 11121:     MqlDateTime d;
P4678: 11122:     TimeToStruct(TC_DayStart(srvNow), d);
P4679: 11123:
P4680: 11124:     datetime lonFromSrv = TC_ZoneToServer(TC_MakeTime(d.year, d.mon, d.day,  2, 0, 0), TZ_NEWYORK);
P4681: 11125:     datetime lonToSrv   = TC_ZoneToServer(TC_MakeTime(d.year, d.mon, d.day,  5, 0, 0), TZ_NEWYORK);
P4682: 11126:     datetime nyFromSrv  = TC_ZoneToServer(TC_MakeTime(d.year, d.mon, d.day,  7, 0, 0), TZ_NEWYORK);
P4683: 11127:     datetime nyToSrv    = TC_ZoneToServer(TC_MakeTime(d.year, d.mon, d.day, 12, 0, 0), TZ_NEWYORK);
P4684: 11128:
P4685: 11129:     PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA][CLOCK] TimeCurrent=%s TimeGMT=%s srvMinusGmt=%+ds "
P4686: 11130:                 "gtc_serverGmtBase=%+ds offsetNow=%s tester=%s",
P4687: 11131:                 TimeToString(srvNow, TIME_DATE|TIME_SECONDS),
P4688: 11132:                 TimeToString(gmtNow, TIME_DATE|TIME_SECONDS),
P4689: 11133:                 (int)((long)srvNow - (long)gmtNow),
P4690: 11134:                 gtc_serverGmtBase,
P4691: 11135:                 TC_ServerOffsetText(),
P4692: 11136:                 (inTester ? "YES" : "NO"));
P4693: 11137:
P4694: 11138:     PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA][CLOCK] session windows resolved to SERVER frame from ET date "
P4695: 11139:                 "%04d.%02d.%02d:  LONDON 02:00-05:00 ET = %s .. %s   |   "
P4696: 11140:                 "NYAM 07:00-12:00 ET = %s .. %s",
P4697: 11141:                 d.year, d.mon, d.day,
P4698: 11142:                 TimeToString(lonFromSrv, TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES),
P4699: 11143:                 TimeToString(lonToSrv,   TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES),
P4700: 11144:                 TimeToString(nyFromSrv,  TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES),
P4701: 11145:                 TimeToString(nyToSrv,    TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES));
P4702: 11146:
P4703: 11147:     if(srvNow <= 0 || gmtNow <= 0)
P4704: 11148:        Print("[SRJ-EA][CLOCK] WARNING: TimeCurrent() or TimeGMT() returned <= 0...");
P4705: 11149:
P4706: 11150:     if(inTester && srvNow == gmtNow)
P4707: 11151:        Print("[SRJ-EA][CLOCK] WARNING: TESTER RUN with TimeGMT()==TimeCurrent()...");
P4708: 11152:    }
P4709: 11153:
P4710: 11154:    ResetLastError();
P4711: 11155:    g_hPoi = iCustom(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, InpPoiMarkerName,
P4712: 11156:                     InpPoi_UseSeed, InpPoi_BinPips, InpPoi_WeightMode);
P4713: 11157:    PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA] POI handle=%d err=%d", g_hPoi, GetLastError());
P4714: 11158:    ResetLastError();
P4715: 11159:    g_hCqd = iCustom(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, InpCqdName,
P4716: 11160:                     PERIOD_D1, InpCqd_NoReset, true,
P4717: 11161:                     InpCqd_MaxCarryBars, InpCqd_MaxBackfillDays);
P4718: 11162:    PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA] CQD handle=%d err=%d", g_hCqd, GetLastError());
P4719: 11163:    ResetLastError();
P4720: 11164:    g_hFlow = iCustom(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, InpFlowLogicName,
P4721: 11165:                      1, InpFL_HtfLookbackBars,
P4722: 11166:                      //--- [P-UJIMPL-IMPL-1 v8 IE1] confirmed selection (F252
P4723: 11167:                      //--- inUseConfirmedHTFOnly; EU preservation sibling row
P4724: 11168:                      //--- grades the global effect).
P4725: 11169:                      PERIOD_H4, PERIOD_H1, PERIOD_M15, true, 60);
P4726: 11170:    PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA] Flow handle=%d err=%d", g_hFlow, GetLastError());
P4727: 11171:     if(g_hPoi == INVALID_HANDLE || g_hCqd == INVALID_HANDLE || g_hFlow == INVALID_HANDLE)
P4728: 11172:       { Print("[SRJ-EA] OnInit FAILED: one or more iCustom handles are invalid."); return INIT_FAILED; }
P4729: 11173:     //--- [P-SEL-1 E51] platform-Fractals handles (own pair; NEVER in the
P4730: 11174:     //--- run-blocking conjunction above - a SEL handle failure reports
P4731: 11175:     //--- SELHALT end-of-run, it never stops the baseline run).
P4732: 11176:     ResetLastError();
P4733: 11177:     g_selfracM5 = iFractals(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT);
P4734: 11178:     PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA] SEL fractal handle M5=%d err=%d", g_selfracM5, GetLastError());
P4735: 11179:     ResetLastError();
P4736: 11180:     g_selfracH1 = iFractals(_Symbol, PERIOD_H1);
P4737: 11181:     PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA] SEL fractal handle H1=%d err=%d", g_selfracH1, GetLastError());
P4738: 11182:     SrjSideNote("INIT", g_dir);
P4739: 11183:     ResetSequence();
P4740: 11184:     //--- [P-SLDEF-1 E13 + amendment, P-SLDEF-4 E34] FRAME_NOTE, once per run:
P4741: 11185:     //--- the six conventions a later session could silently invert. (1) Slot
P4742: 11186:     //--- frame: every printed shift is ReadFlow frame, CopyBuffer position =
P4743: 11187:    //--- every printed shift is ReadFlow frame, CopyBuffer position =
P4744: 11188:    //--- eval shift + FLOW_SHIFT_OFFSET (the settled slot). (2) Apex frame: a
P4745: 11189:    //--- swing value at eval shift s is priced at ApexShift(s) = s + offset;
P4746: 11190:    //--- barTime(s) prints iTime at that price shift - the code's own belief.
P4747: 11191:    //--- (3) Protective sign: raw deltaPts is (ref - today) in points, so
P4748: 11192:    //--- LONG-protective prints negative and SHORT-protective positive;
P4749: 11193:    //--- outwardPts = LONG ? -delta : +delta normalizes to outward-positive.
P4750: 11194:    //--- (4) Population identity [P-NEWS-1 verdict rule]: every gate count
P4751: 11195:    //--- names its population in the same sentence - input-side (guard
P4752: 11196:    //--- applications, raw encounters) vs result-side (post-walk values).
P4753: 11197:     //--- Labels (bar=, barTime) are server time via iTime/TimeToString.
P4754: 11198:     //--- (6) Read windows per reader [P-SLDEF-4 E31]: the ladder reads from
P4755: 11199:     //--- the entry bar to the deepest rung-obligated slot plus margin
P4756: 11200:     //--- (SLADWIN ladWindowStart/ladWindowSpan/ladReadLimit); the OB walk
P4757: 11201:     //--- reads walkStart..walkStart+500, the fractal walk guardStart..
P4758: 11202:     //--- guardStart+500 (SLADWIN walkWinOB/walkWinFR). A ladder window and a
P4759: 11203:     //--- walk window are DIFFERENT windows: coverage is by slot reach, and
P4760: 11204:     //--- ladLimitHit rows are UNCOVERED_READ_LIMIT, never silent.
P4761: 11205:     //--- (7) Origin per site [P-ADOPT-1 E48]: S5 = strict next-open
P4762: 11206:     //--- (caller-stamped entry bar; a stale stamp halts the row, never
P4763: 11207:     //--- substitutes); S2POLL/S3ARM = eval-bar close (the shared
P4764: 11208:     //--- memo-path convention both sites' live R already uses).
P4765: 11209:     //--- THRESHOLD: the live minimum-R is an artifact of the run. Source is
P4766: 11210:    //--- "ini" when the value differs from the compiled default (an ini-set
P4767: 11211:    //--- 1.0 is indistinguishable - recorded as compiled_default).
P4768: 11212:      string frame_note = StringFormat("[SRJ-EA] FRAME_NOTE offset=%d apex=s+%d labels=server-time "
P4769: 11213:                  "protectiveSign=LONG-lower/SHORT-higher outward=LONG(-d)/SHORT(+d) "
P4770: 11214:                  "populations=inputVsResultNamed ladObligN=6refs ladCovers=oblig+ext1 "
P4771: 11215:                  "sigmap=signalTime-s5BarTime-PeriodSeconds "
P4772: 11216:                  "readwin=ladder:entryBar+span|walkOB:walkStart+500|walkFR:guardStart+500 "
P4773: 11217:                  "origin=S5:nextOpen-strictHalt|S2POLL+S3ARM:evalClose "
P4774: 11218:                  "THRESHOLD minRewardRisk=%.2f source=%s",
P4775: 11219:                FLOW_SHIFT_OFFSET, FLOW_SHIFT_OFFSET, InpMinRewardRisk,
P4776: 11220:                ((InpMinRewardRisk == 1.0) ? "compiled_default" : "ini"));
P4777: 11221:    LwAudit("FRAME_NOTE", frame_note);
P4778: 11222:    Print(frame_note);
P4779: 11223:    //--- [P-NEWS-1 E20] pinned-table note, once per run. Digest is a source
P4780: 11224:    //--- string constant (the human check); the BLACKOUT_ROW emissions at the
P4781: 11225:    //--- first bar are the audit artifact. No history needed here.
P4782: 11226:    string table_note = StringFormat("[SRJ-EA] TABLE_NOTE rows=%d digest=%s anchor=%s",
P4783: 11227:                                     SRJ_NEWS_ROWS,
P4784: 11228:                                     "5FFF5C762DABCAB7811C598D8B16942BBCBD5F9D94C91D0BB54CF8C36EF1F134",
P4785: 11229:                                     "21:00_broker");
P4786: 11230:     LwAudit("TABLE_NOTE", table_note);
P4787: 11231:     Print(table_note);
P4788: 11232:     //--- [P-ORIGIN-1 §2/FREEZE] site-origin manifest, once per run.
P4789: 11233:     //--- Candidate bindings resolve ONLY at the seven frozen example
P4790: 11234:     //--- bars (SrjOriginEntry); UNBOUND elsewhere — no fallback, and

P4879: 11323:       //--- DetectPoiRetest (`l <= L-P+EPS && bodyLo >= L-EPS`, SHORT mirror),
P4880: 11324:       //--- POI body-break in the exit census (`bodyLo < L-EPS` /

P4884: 11328:       PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA] N1EQUALS poiEqBody=%d poiEqWick=%d vwapEq=%d pocEq=%d",

P4895: 11339:        //--- [P-SLDEF-1b E15] width audit, once per run per class. truncated

P4915: 11359:                   SRJ_NEWS_ROWS, g_news_nWin, g_news_memberBars, news_expected,

P5121: 11565: //====================== [P-EXITMODEL] exit-phase helpers =============================
P5122: 11566: //--- The section 5.1 body-close trigger set, from the compile-time MT_EXIT_SCOPE,
P5123: 11567: //--- classified through the ruled hierarchy (charter 9.1: AVP-POC over VWAP inside
P5124: 11568: //--- each family; the origin/anchor line's own break always exits - 9.1(2)).
P5125: 11569: bool MtIsBreakTrigger(const int k)
P5126: 11570:   {
P5127: 11571:    if(k == g_mtrade.anchorLine) return true;   // 9.1(2): the origin entry POI
P5128: 11572:    if(MT_EXIT_SCOPE == MT_SCOPE_ALL) return true;
P5129: 11573:    if(MT_EXIT_SCOPE == MT_SCOPE_ANCHOR) return false;
P5130: 11574:    return ((k % 2) == 0);   // FAMILY_POC: the POC buffers are the even indices
P5131: 11575:   }
P5132: 11576:
P5133: 11577: //--- The TP scan replicates ComputeNearestTpTarget's admission EXACTLY but takes the
P5134: 11578: //--- anchor tier from the TRADE's record: the shared function reads the working-set
P5135: 11579: //--- g_anchorLine, which ResetSequence wipes at the signal, and the exit phase runs
P5136: 11580: //--- post-reset. DECLARED DUPLICATION: the entry pipeline is byte-untouched (spec
P5137: 11581: //--- section 7). TpTargetUpdateBest's zone guard (Task 31/Ruling 7c) reads the
P5138: 11582: //--- working-set zone globals, which are 0.0 post-reset, so the guard is inert here -
P5139: 11583: //--- consistent with section 2.2 ("a touch, no geometry") and section 5.3 (no
P5140: 11584: //--- distance/size/width in the exit rules). Q6 ruling honored: re-computed per bar,
P5141: 11585: //--- the nearest valid target, even if less than 1R post-entry.
P5142: 11586: bool MtNearestTpTarget(const int barShift, const ENUM_SRJ_DIR dir,
P5143: 11587:                        const double currentPrice, double &tpTargetOut)
P5144: 11588:   {

P5146: 11590:    bool   haveBest = false;
P5147: 11591:    //--- [P-UJIMPL-IMPL-1 v8 IE5b/IE5c/IE8] election-day key + session names
P5148: 11592:    string uj_dk = UjDayKey(iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift));
P5149: 11593:    const string uj_sname[18] = { "PDH", "PDL", "ASH", "ASL", "LOH", "LOL",
P5150: 11594:                                  "NYH", "NYL", "PMH", "PML",
P5151: 11595:                                  "YASH", "YASL", "YLOH", "YLOL",
P5152: 11596:                                  "YNYH", "YNYL", "YPMH", "YPML" };
P5153: 11597:    //--- [P-UJIMPL-IMPL-1 v8 IE8] one-shot closed-session touch detection.
P5154: 11598:    //--- Levels come from already-closed sessions only (PD NY pair = yesterday
P5155: 11599:    //--- closed; walker pool = strictly older), so the bar-that-closes-the-session
P5156: 11600:    //--- ineligibility holds by construction; lifetime-bound to [fill, now].
P5157: 11601:    double uj_oldTP = g_mtrade.tpRef;
P5158: 11602:    string uj_bk8 = TimeToString(iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift), TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES);
P5159: 11603:    bool uj_firedNow = false;
P5160: 11604:    if(g_mtrade.active && g_mtrade.state == MT_MANAGING && !g_mtrade.uj_touchDone)
P5161: 11605:      {
P5162: 11606:       datetime uj_bt = iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift);
P5163: 11607:       if(uj_bt > 0 && g_mtrade.fillBarTime > 0 && uj_bt > g_mtrade.fillBarTime)
P5164: 11608:         {
P5165: 11609:          double uj_h = iHigh(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift);
P5166: 11610:          double uj_l = iLow(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift);
P5167: 11611:          double uj_o = iOpen(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift);
P5168: 11612:          double uj_c = iClose(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift);
P5169: 11613:          double uj_lv[10]; int uj_ln = 0;
P5170: 11614:          double uj_t = 0.0;
P5171: 11615:          if(ReadFlow(FL_BUF_PD_NY_HIGH, uj_t, barShift) && uj_t != EMPTY_VALUE && uj_t > 0.0 && uj_ln < 10) { uj_lv[uj_ln] = uj_t; uj_ln++; }
P5172: 11616:          if(ReadFlow(FL_BUF_PD_NY_LOW, uj_t, barShift) && uj_t != EMPTY_VALUE && uj_t > 0.0 && uj_ln < 10) { uj_lv[uj_ln] = uj_t; uj_ln++; }
P5173: 11617:          string uj_todayK = UjDayKey(uj_bt);
P5174: 11618:          for(int uj_pi = 0; uj_pi < ArraySize(uj_pool) && uj_ln < 10; uj_pi++)
P5175: 11619:            {
P5176: 11620:             if(uj_pool[uj_pi].dayKey >= uj_todayK) continue;
P5177: 11621:             double uj_pv = uj_pool[uj_pi].value;
P5178: 11622:             if(uj_pv == EMPTY_VALUE || uj_pv <= 0.0) continue;
P5179: 11623:             uj_lv[uj_ln] = uj_pv; uj_ln++;
P5180: 11624:            }
P5181: 11625:          for(int uj_li = 0; uj_li < uj_ln; uj_li++)
P5182: 11626:            {
P5183: 11627:             double uj_L = uj_lv[uj_li];
P5184: 11628:             bool uj_inR = (g_mtrade.dir == DIR_LONG) ? (uj_L > g_mtrade.entryPrice) : (uj_L < g_mtrade.entryPrice);
P5185: 11629:             if(!uj_inR) continue;
P5186: 11630:             if(!(uj_L >= uj_l && uj_L <= uj_h)) continue;
P5187: 11631:             double uj_lo = MathMin(uj_o, uj_c), uj_hi = MathMax(uj_o, uj_c);
P5188: 11632:             string uj_ty = ((uj_L >= uj_lo && uj_L <= uj_hi) ? "PRICE" : "WICK");
P5189: 11633:             g_mtrade.uj_touchDone = true; g_mtrade.uj_touchLevel = uj_L;
P5190: 11634:             g_mtrade.uj_touchType = uj_ty; g_mtrade.uj_touchBarTime = uj_bt;
P5191: 11635:             PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA] UJTOUCH level=%s type=%s event_bar_key=%s admit_bar_key=%s trade_seq=%I64d", DoubleToString(uj_L, _Digits), uj_ty, uj_bk8, TimeToString(g_mtrade.uj_admitBarTime, TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES), g_mtrade.uj_tradeSeq);
P5192: 11636:             uj_firedNow = true; break;
P5193: 11637:            }
P5194: 11638:         }
P5195: 11639:      }
P5196: 11640:    //--- [S1-TP-PROMOTION-001] live promotion: prev-day session H/L join the
P5197: 11641:    //--- candidate walk (indices 10..17 -> swept bits 14..21, unset this stage).
P5198: 11642:    const int sessbufs[18] = { FL_BUF_PDAY_HIGH, FL_BUF_PDAY_LOW,
P5199: 11643:                               FL_BUF_ASIA_HIGH, FL_BUF_ASIA_LOW,
P5200: 11644:                               FL_BUF_LONDON_HIGH, FL_BUF_LONDON_LOW,
P5201: 11645:                               FL_BUF_NY_HIGH, FL_BUF_NY_LOW,
P5202: 11646:                               FL_BUF_PM_HIGH, FL_BUF_PM_LOW,
P5203: 11647:                               FL_BUF_PD_ASIA_HIGH, FL_BUF_PD_ASIA_LOW,
P5204: 11648:                               FL_BUF_PD_LONDON_HIGH, FL_BUF_PD_LONDON_LOW,
P5205: 11649:                               FL_BUF_PD_NY_HIGH, FL_BUF_PD_NY_LOW,
P5206: 11650:                               FL_BUF_PD_PM_HIGH, FL_BUF_PD_PM_LOW };
P5207: 11651:    double s39_mask;
P5208: 11652:    if(!ReadFlow(FL_BUF_SWEPT_MASK, s39_mask, barShift)) s39_mask = EMPTY_VALUE;
P5209: 11653:    for(int i = 0; i < ArraySize(sessbufs); i++)
P5210: 11654:      {
P5211: 11655:       double v;
P5212: 11656:       if(ReadFlow(sessbufs[i], v, barShift) && !TpSessionLevelFiltered(i, s39_mask))
P5213: 11657:          TpTargetUpdateBest(v, dir, currentPrice, best, haveBest, uj_sname[i], uj_dk, -1);
P5214: 11658:      }
P5215: 11659:    //--- [P-UJIMPL-IMPL-1 v8 IE5b] managed-side pool loop (same cache + entry;
P5216: 11660:    //--- tie session > pool > POI; makes always-exit-nearest any-age-true).
P5217: 11661:    if(SrjUjPoolConsumable(uj_dk))
P5218: 11662:      {
P5219: 11663:       for(int uji = 0; uji < ArraySize(uj_pool); uji++)
P5220: 11664:          TpTargetUpdateBest(uj_pool[uji].value, dir, currentPrice, best, haveBest,
P5221: 11665:                             uj_pool[uji].source, uj_pool[uji].dayKey, uj_pool[uji].poolGen);
P5222: 11666:      }
P5223: 11667:    int anchorRank = (g_mtrade.anchorLine >= 0)
P5224: 11668:                     ? g_authorityRank[g_mtrade.anchorLine] : INT_MAX;
P5225: 11669:    for(int k = 0; k < POI_NLINES; k++)
P5226: 11670:      {
P5227: 11671:        if(k == g_mtrade.anchorLine || (g_authorityRank[k] / 2) > (anchorRank / 2))
P5228: 11672:           continue;
P5229: 11673:    if(!UjPoiTargetValid(k, g_mtrade.anchorLine))
P5230: 11674:      { if(InpDebugLog) PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA] UJPOISKIP bar=%s line=%s anchor=%s", TimeToString(iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift), TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES), g_lineCode[k], (g_mtrade.anchorLine >= 0 ? g_lineCode[g_mtrade.anchorLine] : "none")); continue; }
P5231: 11675:        double v;
P5232: 11676:       if(!ReadBuf1(g_hPoi, k, v, barShift)) continue;
P5233: 11677:       TpTargetUpdateBest(v, dir, currentPrice, best, haveBest, g_lineCode[k], uj_dk, -1);
P5234: 11678:      }
P5235: 11679:    if(!haveBest)
P5236: 11680:      {
P5237: 11681:       //--- [P-UJIMPL-IMPL-1 v8 IE8] re-election record: no winner leaves TP
P5238: 11682:       //--- unchanged (explicit no-winner record, one-shot per trade).
P5239: 11683:       if(uj_firedNow)
P5240: 11684:          PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA] UJREELECT bar_key=%s trade_seq=%I64d oldTP=%s newTP=UNCHANGED winner=NONE src=retarget-pool", uj_bk8, g_mtrade.uj_tradeSeq, DoubleToString(uj_oldTP, _Digits));
P5241: 11685:       return false;
P5242: 11686:      }
P5243: 11687:    tpTargetOut = best;
P5244: 11688:    //--- [P-UJIMPL-IMPL-1 v8 IE8] re-election record: election output at the TP
P5245: 11689:    //--- assignment site (one-shot per trade).
P5246: 11690:    if(uj_firedNow)
P5247: 11691:       PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA] UJREELECT bar_key=%s trade_seq=%I64d oldTP=%s newTP=%s winner=%s src=retarget-pool", uj_bk8, g_mtrade.uj_tradeSeq, DoubleToString(uj_oldTP, _Digits), DoubleToString(tpTargetOut, _Digits), uj_winnerSource);
P5248: 11692:    return true;
P5249: 11693:   }
P5250: 11694:
P5251: 11695: //--- [P-SLDEF-2 E25] MTLIFE: one line per managed record at close (rows ==
P5252: 11696: //--- MTEXIT rows exactly; print-only, MTEXIT untouched). openBar = fill
P5253: 11697: //--- candle open ("-" if never filled); verdict/exit from the record.
P5254: 11698: //--- Boundary booleans are lifetime-overlap tests over [fill, exit): news =
P5255: 11699: //--- open when an in-window windowStart falls inside; day/week = open at the
P5256: 11700: //--- census's own 16:55-ET / Friday-17:00-ET marks (the same g_news arrays
P5257: 11701: //--- the edge-triggered census uses). Halt caveat: SrjNewsFinalize runs at
P5258: 11702: //--- end of run, so halt-exclusion is exact iff no in-window row halts
P5259: 11703: //--- (BLACKOUT_HALT count gates it; 0 on every baseline to date).
P5260: 11704: //--- Unevaluable (unfilled/uninit) prints -1.
P5261: 11705: void MtLifeEmit()
P5262: 11706:    {
P5263: 11707:     string mtlOpen = (g_mtrade.fillBarTime > 0) ? TimeToString(g_mtrade.fillBarTime, TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES) : "-";
P5264: 11708:     string mtlClose = (g_mtrade.exitBarTime > 0) ? TimeToString(g_mtrade.exitBarTime, TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES) : "-";
P5265: 11709:     int mtlNews = -1, mtlDay = -1, mtlWeek = -1;
P5266: 11710:     if(g_news_init && g_mtrade.fillBarTime > 0 && g_mtrade.exitBarTime > 0)
P5267: 11711:       {
P5268: 11712:        mtlNews = 0; mtlDay = 0; mtlWeek = 0;
P5269: 11713:        for(int mtlI = 0; mtlI < SRJ_NEWS_ROWS; mtlI++)
P5270: 11714:           if(g_news_inWin[mtlI] == 1 && g_mtrade.fillBarTime <= g_news_winS[mtlI] && g_news_winS[mtlI] < g_mtrade.exitBarTime) { mtlNews = 1; break; }
P5271: 11715:        for(int mtlD = 0; mtlD < g_news_dayN; mtlD++)
P5272: 11716:           if(g_mtrade.fillBarTime <= g_news_dayMarks[mtlD] && g_news_dayMarks[mtlD] < g_mtrade.exitBarTime) { mtlDay = 1; break; }
P5273: 11717:        for(int mtlF = 0; mtlF < g_news_friN; mtlF++)
P5274: 11718:           if(g_mtrade.fillBarTime <= g_news_friMarks[mtlF] && g_news_friMarks[mtlF] < g_mtrade.exitBarTime) { mtlWeek = 1; break; }
P5275: 11719:       }
P5276: 11720:     string mtlLine = StringFormat("[SRJ-EA] MTLIFE fields=11 openBar=%s dir=%s entry=%s sl=%s tp=%s verdict=%s closeBar=%s closePx=%s openAtNewsStart=%d openAtDayClose=%d openAtWeekClose=%d",
P5277: 11721:               mtlOpen, DirName(g_mtrade.dir),
P5278: 11722:               DoubleToString(g_mtrade.entryPrice, _Digits),
P5279: 11723:               DoubleToString(g_mtrade.slRef, _Digits),
P5280: 11724:               DoubleToString(g_mtrade.tpRef, _Digits),
P5281: 11725:               MtExitName(g_mtrade.exitReason),
P5282: 11726:               mtlClose, DoubleToString(g_mtrade.exitPrice, _Digits),
P5283: 11727:               mtlNews, mtlDay, mtlWeek);
P5284: 11728:     LwAudit("MTLIFE", mtlLine);
P5285: 11729:     Print(mtlLine);
P5286: 11730:    }
P5287: 11731:
P5288: 11732: //--- [P-SLDEF-3 E29] MTFLIP: one line per HTF_FLIP verdict evaluation
P5289: 11733: //--- (print-only; the exit below is untouched). openBar = fill-bar shift
P5290: 11734: //--- (-1 unevaluable); biasBefore = anti-leg count on the previous bar
P5291: 11735: //--- (-1 unreadable); flipSourceBar = the eval bar when the flip is new,
P5292: 11736: //--- else the previous bar (first KNOWN flipped bar — a lower bound on the
P5293: 11737: //--- flip's age, disclosed). barsHeld from the two shifts (0 = the flip was
P5294: 11738: //--- evaluable against the record's own opening bar).
P5295: 11739: void MtFlipEmit(const int barShift, const datetime barTime, const int antiNow, const int want)
P5296: 11740:    {
P5297: 11741:     double fH = 0.0, fM = 0.0, fL = 0.0;
P5298: 11742:     int antiPrev = -1;
P5299: 11743:     if(ReadFlow(FL_BUF_HTF_HIGH, fH, barShift + 1) && ReadFlow(FL_BUF_HTF_MID, fM, barShift + 1) && ReadFlow(FL_BUF_HTF_LOW, fL, barShift + 1))
P5300: 11744:       {
P5301: 11745:        antiPrev = 0;
P5302: 11746:        if((int)MathRound(fH) == -want) antiPrev++;
P5303: 11747:        if((int)MathRound(fM) == -want) antiPrev++;
P5304: 11748:        if((int)MathRound(fL) == -want) antiPrev++;
P5305: 11749:       }
P5306: 11750:     int fillShift = (g_mtrade.fillBarTime > 0) ? iBarShift(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, g_mtrade.fillBarTime, false) : -1;
P5307: 11751:     int barsHeld = (fillShift >= 0) ? (fillShift - barShift) : -1;
P5308: 11752:     int sameBar = (barsHeld == 0) ? 1 : ((barsHeld < 0) ? -1 : 0);
P5309: 11753:     datetime prevT = iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift + 1);
P5310: 11754:     datetime flipSrc = (antiPrev >= 0 && antiPrev < 2) ? barTime : prevT;
P5311: 11755:     string mfl = StringFormat("[SRJ-EA] MTFLIP fields=10 openBar=%d openBarTime=%s entry=%s evalBar=%d evalBarTime=%s biasBefore=%d biasAfter=%d flipSourceBar=%s barsHeld=%d sameBarFlip=%d",
P5312: 11756:               fillShift,
P5313: 11757:               (g_mtrade.fillBarTime > 0) ? TimeToString(g_mtrade.fillBarTime, TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES) : "-",
P5314: 11758:               DoubleToString(g_mtrade.entryPrice, _Digits),
P5315: 11759:               barShift, TimeToString(barTime, TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES),
P5316: 11760:               antiPrev, antiNow,
P5317: 11761:               TimeToString(flipSrc, TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES),
P5318: 11762:               barsHeld, sameBar);

P5340: 11834: // Spec section 4 site 3: the exit, evaluated at the NEXT candle's open. Called once
P5341: 11835: // per closed bar from OnTick AFTER the entry pipeline (section 7's separation: this
P5342: 11836: // function never touches the entry pipeline or any working-set field). Every verdict
P5343: 11837: // is logged (instrumentation-first, section 4's mitigation); an actual exit also
P5344: 11838: // emits the EXIT alert (ALERT-ONLY preserved - never an order). Same-bar priority
P5345: 11839: // when several tests fire together: SL, then TP_TOUCH, then POI_BODY_BREAK, then
P5346: 11840: // HTF_FLIP (when re-enabled; beats DAY_CLOSE on shared bars), then DAY_CLOSE
P5347: 11841: // (universal scope: every managed trade) (the conservative stop-first standard; MTEXIT/MTLIFE record terminal
P5348: 11842: // exit reasons so the operator can re-judge any instance).

P5350: ```mql5
P5351: 11851:    double o = iOpen(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift);

P5361: 11861:    double EPS = 0.001 * _Point;   // the T161K float guard, threshold-free semantics
P5362: 11862:
P5363: 11863:    //--- PENDING_FILL (section 5.5): fill on the first touch of the entry level.
P5364: 11864:    //--- Under the next-open entry the fill bar's own open IS the entry, so this
P5365: 11865:    //--- fills at the first evaluation; the branch keeps the lifecycle complete.
P5366: 11866:    if(g_mtrade.state == MT_PENDING_FILL)
P5367: 11867:      {

P5369: 11869:                                                 : (h >= g_mtrade.entryPrice - EPS);
P5370: 11870:       if(!touched)

P5379: 11879:               {

P5381: 11881:                g_mtrade.exitReason = MT_EXIT_CANCEL_BIAS;

P5383: 11883:                g_mtrade.exitPrice   = nextOpenPx;

P5591: **EX-8. Closed-session helper with the admission-containment test at EA 1927 - EA 1893-1930.**
P5592:
P5593: ```mql5
P5594: 1893: bool UjClosedSessionTarget(const SManagedTrade &t, const int barShift, double &px)
P5595: 1894:    {
P5596: 1895:     px = 0.0;
P5597: 1896:     if(t.dir != DIR_LONG && t.dir != DIR_SHORT) return false;
P5598: 1897:     int es = t.sessionAtEntry;
P5599: 1898:     if(es != SESSION_LONDON && es != SESSION_NYAM) return false;
P5600: 1899:     if(t.uj_admitBarTime <= 0) return false;
P5601: 1900:      datetime bt = iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift);
P5602: 1901:      if(bt == 0) return false;
P5603: 1902:      if((int)CurrentTradingWindow(bt) == es) return false;
P5604: 1903:      datetime bp = iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift + 1);
P5605: 1904:      if(bp == 0) return false;
P5606: 1905:      if((int)CurrentTradingWindow(bp) != es) return false;  // first-out-bar pin (V347 Luna-2): barShift must be the first bar outside the entry-session run
P5607: 1906:      double ext = 0.0;
P5608: 1907:     bool have = false;
P5609: 1908:     bool uj_ended = false;
P5610: 1909:     datetime uj_newest = 0;
P5611: 1910:     datetime uj_oldest = 0;
P5612: 1911:      for(int k = barShift + 1; k < barShift + 601; k++)
P5613: 1912:        {
P5614: 1913:         datetime bk = iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, k);
P5615: 1914:         if(bk == 0) break;
P5616: 1915:         if((int)CurrentTradingWindow(bk) != es)
P5617: 1916:           {
P5618: 1917:            if(have) uj_ended = true;
P5619: 1918:            break;  // contiguous-run end (V347 Luna-2): the walk starts inside an es run by the barShift+1 pin; admission containment is the separate P097 test, so any non-es bar ends the walk - fail-closed when no price seen yet
P5620: 1919:           }
P5621: 1920:        if(uj_newest == 0) { uj_newest = bk; uj_oldest = bk; }
P5622: 1921:        else uj_oldest = bk;
P5623: 1922:        double v = (t.dir == DIR_LONG) ? iHigh(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, k) : iLow(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, k);
P5624: 1923:        if(v <= 0.0) continue;
P5625: 1924:        if(!have || (t.dir == DIR_LONG && v > ext) || (t.dir == DIR_SHORT && v < ext)) { ext = v; have = true; }
P5626: 1925:       }
P5627: 1926:     if(!have || !uj_ended || ext <= 0.0) return false;
P5628: 1927:     if(t.uj_admitBarTime < uj_oldest || t.uj_admitBarTime > uj_newest) return false;
P5629: 1928:     px = ext;
P5630: 1929:     return true;
P5631: 1930:    }
P5632: ```

P5781: 11810:                   DoubleToString(refPx, _Digits));

P5798: 12070:
P5799: 12071: //====================== [P-UJIMPL-IMPL-1 v8] UJ closure implementation ============
P5800: 12072: //--- print helper: EMPTY-safe double
P5801: 12073: string UjDbl(double v)
P5802: 12074:   {
P5803: 12075:    if(v == EMPTY_VALUE) return "EMPTY";
P5804: 12076:    return DoubleToString(v, 1);
P5805: 12077:   }
P5806: 12078: //--- day key YYYY.MM.DD at broker midnight (F327-328 alignment, EA-side)
P5807: 12079: string UjDayKey(datetime t)
P5808: 12080:   {
P5809: 12081:    if(t <= 0) return "";
P5810: 12082:    return TimeToString(StringToTime(TimeToString(t, TIME_DATE)), TIME_DATE);
P5811: 12083:   }
P5812: 12084: //--- whole-day difference bar-day minus key-day (-1 when unkeyed)
P5813: 12085: int UjDayDiff(datetime barT, string dayKey)
P5814: 12086:   {
P5815: 12087:    if(barT <= 0 || dayKey == "") return -1;
P5816: 12088:    return (int)((StringToTime(TimeToString(barT, TIME_DATE)) - StringToTime(dayKey)) / 86400);
P5817: 12089:   }
P5818: 12090: //--- shared 1R assertion (single print schema; callers abort on false)
P5819: 12091: bool SrjUjAssert1R(double entry, double sl, double tp, string barKey, string src,
P5820: 12092:                    double &riskOut, double &rewardOut, double &ROut)
P5821: 12093:   {
P5822: 12094:    int d = 0;
P5823: 12095:    if(tp > entry && sl < entry) d = 1;
P5824: 12096:    else if(tp < entry && sl > entry) d = -1;
P5825: 12097:    double risk = -1.0, reward = -1.0, R = -1.0;
P5826: 12098:    bool ok = false;
P5827: 12099:    if(d != 0)
P5828: 12100:      {
P5829: 12101:       risk = (d == 1) ? (entry - sl) : (sl - entry);
P5830: 12102:       reward = (d == 1) ? (tp - entry) : (entry - tp);
P5831: 12103:       R = (risk > 0.0) ? (reward / risk) : -1.0;
P5832: 12104:       ok = (risk > 0.0 && reward > 0.0 && reward >= risk);
P5833: 12105:      }
P5834: 12106:    PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA] UJ1R bar=%s src=%s entry=%s sl=%s tp=%s risk=%s reward=%s R=%.2f verdict=%s",
P5835: 12107:                barKey, src, DoubleToString(entry, _Digits), DoubleToString(sl, _Digits),
P5836: 12108:                DoubleToString(tp, _Digits), DoubleToString(risk, _Digits),
P5837: 12109:                DoubleToString(reward, _Digits), R, (ok ? "PASS" : "FAIL"));
P5838: 12110:    riskOut = risk; rewardOut = reward; ROut = R;
P5839: 12111:    return ok;
P5840: 12112:   }
P5841: 12113: //--- pool consumability: READY for the election day (SHORT/EMPTY/FAILED/stale: no-consume)
P5842: 12114: bool SrjUjPoolConsumable(string dayKey)
P5843: 12115:   {
P5844: 12116:    return (uj_pubState == UJ_POOL_READY && dayKey != "" && uj_poolDayKey == dayKey);
P5845: 12117:   }
P5846: 12118: //--- history walker: builds temp pool over [2026.04.29, today]
P5847: 12119: int SrjHistPoolBuild(SUjPoolRec &out[], string &achStart, int &dayCnt, int &famRead, int &unavail, int &emptyValid)
P5848: 12120:   {
P5849: 12121:    ArrayResize(out, 0);
P5850: 12122:    achStart = ""; dayCnt = 0; famRead = 0; unavail = 0; emptyValid = 0;
P5851: 12123:    datetime reqD = StringToTime("2026.04.29");
P5852: 12124:    datetime nowB = iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, 1);
P5853: 12125:    if(nowB <= 0 || reqD <= 0) return UJ_POOL_FAILED;
P5854: 12126:    datetime todayD = StringToTime(TimeToString(nowB, TIME_DATE));
P5855: 12127:    datetime maxOrigin = todayD - 2 * 86400;
P5856: 12128:    string sessName[4] = { "ASIA", "LONDON", "NY", "PM" };
P5857: 12129:    bool truncated = false;
P5858: 12130:    for(datetime D = reqD; D <= todayD; D += 86400)
P5859: 12131:      {
P5860: 12132:       datetime refT = D + 43200;
P5861: 12133:       int sh = iBarShift(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, refT, false);
P5862: 12134:       if(sh < 0) { truncated = true; unavail++; break; }
P5863: 12135:       datetime bt = iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, sh);
P5864: 12136:       if(bt <= 0) { truncated = true; unavail++; break; }
P5865: 12137:       if(StringToTime(TimeToString(bt, TIME_DATE)) != D) continue;
P5866: 12138:       dayCnt++;
P5867: 12139:       if(achStart == "") achStart = TimeToString(D, TIME_DATE);
P5868: 12140:       bool dayEmpty = true;
P5869: 12141:       for(int s = 0; s < 4; s++)
P5870: 12142:         {
P5871: 12143:          double vLH = 0.0, vLL = 0.0, vPH = 0.0, vPL = 0.0;
P5872: 12144:          if(!ReadFlow(10 + s * 2, vLH, sh) || !ReadFlow(11 + s * 2, vLL, sh) ||
P5873: 12145:             !ReadFlow(40 + s * 2, vPH, sh) || !ReadFlow(41 + s * 2, vPL, sh))
P5874: 12146:            { truncated = true; unavail++; break; }
P5875: 12147:          famRead += 4;
P5876: 12148:          double lv[4]; datetime ov[4]; string sv[4];
P5877: 12149:          lv[0] = vLH; ov[0] = D; sv[0] = "LIVE";
P5878: 12150:          lv[1] = vLL; ov[1] = D; sv[1] = "LIVE";
P5879: 12151:          lv[2] = vPH; ov[2] = D - 86400; sv[2] = "PD";
P5880: 12152:          lv[3] = vPL; ov[3] = D - 86400; sv[3] = "PD";
P5881: 12153:          for(int r = 0; r < 4; r++)
P5882: 12154:            {
P5883: 12155:             if(lv[r] == EMPTY_VALUE || lv[r] <= 0.0) continue;
P5884: 12156:             if(ov[r] < reqD || ov[r] > maxOrigin) continue;
P5885: 12157:             int side = ((r == 0 || r == 2) ? 0 : 1);
P5886: 12158:             string okey = TimeToString(ov[r], TIME_DATE);
P5887: 12159:             bool dup = false;
P5888: 12160:             for(int e = 0; e < ArraySize(out); e++)
P5889: 12161:               { if(out[e].dayKey == okey && out[e].sess == sessName[s] && out[e].side == side) { dup = true; break; } }
P5890: 12162:             if(dup) continue;
P5891: 12163:             int n = ArraySize(out);
P5892: 12164:             ArrayResize(out, n + 1);
P5893: 12165:             out[n].dayKey = okey; out[n].sess = sessName[s]; out[n].side = side;
P5894: 12166:             out[n].value = lv[r]; out[n].source = sv[r];
P5895: 12167:             out[n].closure = ov[r] + 86399; out[n].poolGen = uj_poolGen + 1;
P5896: 12168:             dayEmpty = false;
P5897: 12169:            }
P5898: 12170:          if(truncated) break;
P5899: 12171:         }
P5900: 12172:       if(truncated) break;
P5901: 12173:       if(dayEmpty) emptyValid++;
P5902: 12174:      }
P5903: 12175:    if(truncated) return (ArraySize(out) > 0 ? UJ_POOL_SHORT : UJ_POOL_FAILED);
P5904: 12176:    return UJ_POOL_READY;
P5905: 12177:   }
P5906: 12178: //--- unconditional refresh in the new-bar path (day-keyed rollover rebuild)
P5907: 12179: void SrjUjPoolRefresh()
P5908: 12180:   {
P5909: 12181:    datetime nowB = iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, 1);
P5910: 12182:    string todayK = UjDayKey(nowB);
P5911: 12183:    if(todayK != "" && todayK == uj_poolDayKey && uj_pubState == UJ_POOL_READY)
P5912: 12184:      {
P5913: 12185:       if(InpDebugLog)
P5914: 12186:          PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA] UJPOOLCOV req=%s ach=%s days=%d famRead=%d unavail=%d emptyValid=%d state=%d attempt=%d poolGen=%d cadence=no-rebuild",
P5915: 12187:                      uj_reqStart, uj_achStart, uj_dayCount, uj_famRead, uj_unavail,
P5916: 12188:                      uj_emptyValid, uj_pubState, uj_attemptCount, uj_poolGen);
P5917: 12189:       return;
P5918: 12190:      }
P5919: 12191:    uj_attemptState = UJ_POOL_BUILDING; uj_attemptCount++;
P5920: 12192:    SUjPoolRec tmp[];
P5921: 12193:    string ach = ""; int dc = 0, fr = 0, un = 0, ev = 0;
P5922: 12194:    int st = SrjHistPoolBuild(tmp, ach, dc, fr, un, ev);
P5923: 12195:    uj_attemptState = st;
P5924: 12196:    uj_reqStart = "2026.04.29"; uj_achStart = ach;
P5925: 12197:    uj_dayCount = dc; uj_famRead = fr; uj_unavail = un; uj_emptyValid = ev;
P5926: 12198:    if(st == UJ_POOL_READY)
P5927: 12199:      {
P5928: 12200:       int n = ArraySize(tmp);
P5929: 12201:       ArrayResize(uj_pool, n);
P5930: 12202:       for(int i = 0; i < n; i++)
P5931: 12203:         {
P5932: 12204:          uj_pool[i].dayKey = tmp[i].dayKey; uj_pool[i].sess = tmp[i].sess;
P5933: 12205:          uj_pool[i].side = tmp[i].side; uj_pool[i].value = tmp[i].value;
P5934: 12206:          uj_pool[i].source = tmp[i].source; uj_pool[i].closure = tmp[i].closure;
P5935: 12207:          uj_pool[i].poolGen = tmp[i].poolGen;
P5936: 12208:         }
P5937: 12209:       uj_poolDayKey = todayK; uj_pubState = UJ_POOL_READY; uj_poolGen++;
P5938: 12210:      }
P5939: 12211:    //--- SHORT/FAILED: published pool untouched (last-good; stale never consumed:
P5940: 12212:    //--- the consumability gate requires day match + READY).
P5941: 12213:    if(InpDebugLog)
P5942: 12214:       PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA] UJPOOLCOV req=%s ach=%s days=%d famRead=%d unavail=%d emptyValid=%d state=%d attempt=%d poolGen=%d cadence=rebuild",
P5943: 12215:                   uj_reqStart, ach, dc, fr, un, ev, st, uj_attemptCount, uj_poolGen);
P5944: 12216:    if(InpDebugLog)
P5945: 12217:       PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA] UJPOOLSTATE dayKey=%s publishedPoolState=%d refreshAttemptState=%d poolGen=%d",
P5946: 12218:                   uj_poolDayKey, uj_pubState, uj_attemptState, uj_poolGen);
P5947: 12219:   }
P5948: 12220: //--- run-level pool-service record (zero admissions print the schema, never vacuous)
P5949: 12221: void SrjUjPoolFinalize()
P5950: 12222:   {
P5951: 12223:    if(!InpDebugLog) return;
P5952: 12224:    datetime nowB = iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, 1);
P5953: 12225:    PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA] UJPOOLSVC requestedStart=%s achievedStart=%s runEnd=%s dayCount=%d familyRead=%d unavailable=%d emptyValid=%d finalState=%d attempt=%d poolGen=%d admissions=%d",
P5954: 12226:                uj_reqStart, uj_achStart, UjDayKey(nowB), uj_dayCount, uj_famRead,
P5955: 12227:                uj_unavail, uj_emptyValid, uj_attemptState, uj_attemptCount, uj_poolGen, uj_admitCount);
P5956: 12228:   }
P5957: 12229: //--- single probe printer (pass bar-time join key; M15 row gated on M15-new-bar)
P5958: 12230: void SrjUjProbeTuple(int barShift, datetime barTime)
P5959: 12231:   {
P5960: 12232:    string bk = TimeToString(barTime, TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES);
P5961: 12233:    double h4 = EMPTY_VALUE, h1 = EMPTY_VALUE, m15 = EMPTY_VALUE, ltf = EMPTY_VALUE;
P5962: 12234:    if(!ReadFlow(FL_BUF_HTF_HIGH, h4, barShift)) h4 = EMPTY_VALUE;
P5963: 12235:    if(!ReadFlow(FL_BUF_HTF_MID, h1, barShift)) h1 = EMPTY_VALUE;
P5964: 12236:    if(!ReadFlow(FL_BUF_HTF_LOW, m15, barShift)) m15 = EMPTY_VALUE;
P5965: 12237:    if(!ReadFlow(FL_BUF_LTF_BIAS, ltf, barShift)) ltf = EMPTY_VALUE;
P5966: 12238:    //--- probe-side DIV classifier: verbatim firing-walk bound, full domain
P5967: 12239:    int maxWalk = Bars(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT) - 1;
P5968: 12240:    int readFail = 0, emptyV = 0, zeroV = 0, latestNZ = 0;
P5969: 12241:    string kind = "-";
P5970: 12242:    bool complete = true;
P5971: 12243:    for(int s = barShift; s <= maxWalk; s++)
P5972: 12244:      {
P5973: 12245:       double verdict = EMPTY_VALUE;

P6001: 12273: //====================== OnTick =========================================
P6002: 12274: void OnTick()
P6003: 12275:   {
P6004: 12276:    static datetime s_lastBarTime = 0;
P6005: 12277:    datetime currentBarTime = iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, 1);
P6006: 12278:    if(currentBarTime == s_lastBarTime) return;
P6007: 12279:    s_lastBarTime = currentBarTime;
P6008: 12280:    //--- [P-UJIMPL-IMPL-1 v8 IE5] unconditional pool refresh in the new-bar path
P6009: 12281:    SrjUjPoolRefresh();
P6010: 12282:    LoadWorkingSet(1, currentBarTime);
P6011: 12283:    //--- [P-UJIMPL-IMPL-1 v8 IE9/IE4] boundary order: memo-clear, probe-print,
P6012: 12284:    //--- then evaluation (non-load-bearing beyond this sentence).
P6013: 12285:    uj_memo_valid = false; uj_memo_barTime = 0; uj_memo_src = "";
P6014: 12286:    SrjUjProbeTuple(1, currentBarTime);
P6015: 12287:    EvaluateClosedBar(1, currentBarTime);
P6016: 12288:    StoreWorkingSet(1, currentBarTime);
P6017: 12289:    //--- [P-EXITMODEL] the section 4 site-3 exit phase: runs AFTER the entry pipeline
P6018: 12290:    //--- and AFTER the working-set store (it touches NO working-set field - section 7
P6019: 12291:    //--- separation). Evaluates the managed trade at the NEXT candle's open.
P6020: 12292:    EvaluateManagedTrade(1);
P6021: 12293:    //--- [P-NEWS-1 E22] blackout census hook, last: observes the settled state.
P6022: 12294:    SrjNewsInit();
P6023: 12295:    if(InpDebugLog && SHADOW_NEWS)
P6024: 12296:       SrjNewsOnBar(currentBarTime);
P6025: 12297:   }
P6026: 12298: //+------------------------------------------------------------------+
P6027: ```

P6276: 6830: bool FindLegTouch(int barShift, double zHi, double zLo,
P6277: 6831:                   int &foundShiftOut, double &legTimeOut, bool fromFvg)
P6278: 6832:   {
P6279: 6833:    foundShiftOut = -1;
P6280: 6834:    legTimeOut    = 0.0;
P6281: 6835:    if(!(zHi > 0.0 && zLo > 0.0)) return false;
P6282: 6836:
P6283: 6837:    double legT;
P6284: 6838:    if(ReadFlow(FL_BUF_STRUCT_LEG_TIME, legT, barShift) &&
P6285: 6839:       legT > 0.0 && legT != EMPTY_VALUE)
P6286: 6840:       legTimeOut = legT;
P6287: 6841:    datetime legBoundary = (legTimeOut > 0.0) ? (datetime)legTimeOut : 0;
P6288: 6842:
P6289: 6843:    for(int s = barShift; s <= barShift + 500; s++)
P6290: 6844:      {
P6291: 6845:       datetime bt = iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, s);
P6292: 6846:       if(bt <= 0)                              break;
P6293: 6847:       if(legBoundary > 0 && bt < legBoundary)   break;
P6294: 6848:
P6295: 6849:       double so = iOpen (_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, s);
P6296: 6850:       double sh = iHigh (_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, s);
P6297: 6851:       double sl = iLow  (_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, s);
P6298: 6852:       double sc = iClose(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, s);
P6299: 6853:
P6300: 6854:       bool oppositeDir = (g_dir == DIR_LONG) ? (sc < so) : (sc > so);
P6301: 6855:       bool touchesZone = (sh >= zLo && sl <= zHi);
P6302: 6856:       if(oppositeDir && (!fromFvg || touchesZone))
P6303: 6857:         { foundShiftOut = s; return true; }
P6304: 6858:      }
P6305: 6859:    return false;
P6306: 6860:   }

P6308: 6862: //====================== [Task 55 / EA-56 + EA-58] Zone in-play test ===
P6309: 6863: //--- Ruling 8's in-play test, extracted so it can be applied to MORE THAN ONE
P6310: 6864: //--- candidate zone on the same bar. Logic is character-equivalent to the test
P6311: 6865: //--- the S3 block already runs inline: BAR penetration of the zone by the
P6312: 6866: //--- evaluation bar's own range, else SWING1 containment (freshest confirmed
P6313: 6867: //--- swing on the protective side), else SWING2 containment (next DISTINCT
P6314: 6868: //--- confirmed swing on that same side).
P6315: 6869: //---
P6316: 6870: //--- No distance parameter, no bar-count parameter, no tolerance. The _Point
P6317: 6871: //--- separation test and the 500-slot bound match FindNearestSwing, the 2-swing
P6318: 6872: //--- stop branch and the S3 inline test exactly; they are safety limits, not
P6319: 6873: //--- tunable thresholds, so Part A section 7 is not engaged.
P6320: 6874: //---
P6321: 6875: //--- Silent by design. The caller reports the verdicts (see ZONEPICK), so this
P6322: 6876: //--- adds no per-bar journal output of its own.
P6323: 6877: //---
P6324: 6878: //--- The S3 block's inline copy and ZoneAdoptable's copy are NOT removed. That
P6325: 6879: //--- consolidation is EA-49 and belongs to the state-machine rewrite.
P6326: 6880: bool ZoneInPlay(int barShift, double zHi, double zLo,
P6327: 6881:                 double stopRef, bool haveStop)
P6328: 6882:   {
P6329: 6883:    if(!(zHi > 0.0 && zLo > 0.0)) return false;
P6330: 6884:
P6331: 6885:    double bHi = iHigh(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift);
P6332: 6886:    double bLo = iLow (_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift);
P6333: 6887:    if(bHi >= zLo && bLo <= zHi) return true;
P6334: 6888:
P6335: 6889:    int    buf = (g_dir == DIR_LONG) ? FL_BUF_SWING_LOW : FL_BUF_SWING_HIGH;
P6336: 6890:    double sw1 = 0.0;
P6337: 6891:    int    sh1 = -1;
P6338: 6892:    if(!FindNearestSwing(buf, barShift, sw1, sh1)) return false;
P6339: 6893:    if(sw1 >= zLo && sw1 <= zHi) return true;
P6340: 6894:
P6341: 6895:    //--- [STEP 1 / charter ruling 3] In-play depth is the SL LEG: every confirmed
P6342: 6896:    //--- protective-side swing from the evaluation bar back to the stop reference
P6343: 6897:    //--- chosen by ComputeSlReference. The stop swing itself is tested and then
P6344: 6898:    //--- terminates the walk - the one verified operator zone was put in play by
P6345: 6899:    //--- the second swing, the one the stop was placed at. Without a stop
P6346: 6900:    //--- reference this bar, the measured two-swing depth (SWING2) remains the
P6347: 6901:    //--- bound, per council Part 1.1. No distance parameter; the bounds are the
P6348: 6902:    //--- stop reference (structural), history exhaustion (bt<=0), and the _Point
P6349: 6903:     //--- distinctness test - safety limits and structure, never thresholds.
P6350: 6904:     //--- [P-TRIM-S2POLL E3] loop-invariant hoist. Bars() cannot change within one
P6351: 6905:     //--- EvaluateClosedBar pass. Matches the existing t127_limit2 / t133_limit idiom.
P6352: 6906:     const int zip_limit = barShift + Bars(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT);
P6353: 6907:     if(!haveStop)
P6354: 6908:      {
P6355: 6909:       for(int s = sh1 + 1; s <= sh1 + 500; s++)
P6356: 6910:         {
P6357: 6911:          double v2;
P6358: 6912:          if(!ReadFlow(buf, v2, s))          break;
P6359: 6913:          if(v2 == EMPTY_VALUE || v2 <= 0.0) continue;
P6360: 6914:          if(MathAbs(v2 - sw1) <= _Point)    continue;
P6361: 6915:          return (v2 >= zLo && v2 <= zHi);
P6362: 6916:         }
P6363: 6917:       return false;
P6364: 6918:      }
P6365: 6919:     double prev = sw1;
P6366: 6920:     for(int s = sh1 + 1; s <= zip_limit; s++)
P6367: ```
P6368: ```mql5
P6369: 6654: bool ReadQualifyingZone(int barShift, double &zHiOut, double &zLoOut, bool &fromFvgOut,
P6370: 6655:                         double stopRef, bool haveStop)
P6371: 6656:   {
P6372: 6657:    double xobHi = 0.0, xobLo = 0.0, fvgHi = 0.0, fvgLo = 0.0;
P6373: 6658:
P6374: 6659:    // [Task 105] Identity census at the S4 re-read site. Placed immediately
P6375: 6660:    // after the zone locals are declared and before any read or return, so it
P6376: 6661:    // cannot alter the function's result on any path. Prints ids only; the
P6377: 6662:    // bounds for this bar are already printed by ZONEMOVE and ZONEADOPT, so
P6378: 6663:    // cross-referencing by bar= is sufficient and no duplicated read is needed.
P6379: 6664:    // -1 means the buffer read failed. 0 means FlowLogic selected no object.
P6380: 6665:    double t105a_xobId = -1.0, t105a_fvgId = -1.0;
P6381: 6666:    if(!ReadFlow(FL_BUF_XOB_OBJ_ID, t105a_xobId, barShift)) t105a_xobId = -1.0;
P6382: 6667:    if(!ReadFlow(FL_BUF_FVG_OBJ_ID, t105a_fvgId, barShift)) t105a_fvgId = -1.0;
P6383: 6668:    if(InpDebugLog)
P6384: 6669:       PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA] ZONEID bar=%s site=S4RQZ xobId=%d fvgId=%d",
P6385: 6670:                   TimeToString(iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift), TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES),
P6386: 6671:                   (int)t105a_xobId, (int)t105a_fvgId);
P6387: 6672:
P6388: 6673:    bool haveXob = ReadFlow(FL_BUF_XOB_ZONE_HIGH, xobHi, barShift) && xobHi != EMPTY_VALUE &&
P6389: 6674:                   ReadFlow(FL_BUF_XOB_ZONE_LOW,  xobLo, barShift) && xobLo != EMPTY_VALUE;
P6390: 6675:    bool haveFvg = ReadFlow(FL_BUF_FVG_LEG_ZONE_HIGH, fvgHi, barShift) && fvgHi != EMPTY_VALUE &&
P6391: 6676:                   ReadFlow(FL_BUF_FVG_LEG_ZONE_LOW,  fvgLo, barShift) && fvgLo != EMPTY_VALUE;
P6392: 6677:
P6393: 6678:    //--- [STEP 1 / council Part 2.2] This site previously returned the FVG
P6394: 6679:    //--- unconditionally, with no in-play test applied here. With the widened walk
P6395: 6680:    //--- live at S3, a not-in-play FVG could displace an in-play XOB on this
P6396: 6681:    //--- re-read, making the widened admission reversible one bar later. Fix:
P6397: 6682:    //--- when BOTH zones are exported, the FVG yields to an in-play XOB exactly
P6398: 6683:    //--- when the FVG itself is not in play. Strictly monotone: every bar that
P6399: 6684:    //--- returned the XOB before still does; a lone FVG still returns as before.
P6400: 6685:    if(haveFvg && haveXob)
P6401: 6686:      {
P6402: 6687:       bool fvgInPlay = ZoneInPlay(barShift, MathMax(fvgHi, fvgLo), MathMin(fvgHi, fvgLo), stopRef, haveStop);
P6403: 6688:       bool xobInPlay = ZoneInPlay(barShift, MathMax(xobHi, xobLo), MathMin(xobHi, xobLo), stopRef, haveStop);
P6404: 6689:       if(InpDebugLog)
P6405: 6690:          PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA] RQZPICK bar=%s site=S4RQZ fvgInPlay=%d xobInPlay=%d downgraded=%d",
P6406: 6691:                      TimeToString(iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift), TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES),
P6407: 6692:                      (int)fvgInPlay, (int)xobInPlay,
P6408: 6693:                      (int)((!fvgInPlay && xobInPlay) ? 1 : 0));
P6409: 6694:       if(!fvgInPlay && xobInPlay)
P6410: 6695:         { zHiOut = MathMax(xobHi, xobLo); zLoOut = MathMin(xobHi, xobLo); fromFvgOut = false; return true; }
P6411: 6696:      }
P6412: 6697:    if(haveFvg)
P6413: 6698:      { zHiOut = MathMax(fvgHi, fvgLo); zLoOut = MathMin(fvgHi, fvgLo); fromFvgOut = true;  return true; }
P6414: 6699:    if(haveXob)
P6415: 6700:      { zHiOut = MathMax(xobHi, xobLo); zLoOut = MathMin(xobHi, xobLo); fromFvgOut = false; return true; }
P6416: 6701:    return false;
P6417: 6702:   }
P6418: 6703:
P6419: 6704: //====================== [Task 36 / EA-47] Zone adoption guard =========
P6420: 6705: //--- Measured cause: on 2026.08.20 three of six ZONEMOVEs adopted
P6421: 6706: //--- 1.16547-1.16595 with obSwing=1.16536 - byte-identical to 08.19's values.
P6422: 6707: //--- SRJ_NearestPromotedOBIndex selects the largest startBar among promoted,
P6423: 6708: //--- valid, activated in-bias order blocks; when nothing from the current day
P6424: 6709: //--- qualifies it returns the previous day's. One of those adoptions cleared a
P6425: 6710: //--- legitimate touch (TOUCHCLEAR 18:55). Part A Step 4 requires "the current,
P6426: 6711: //--- freshest structure", so a zone ~200 points from price with no structure
P6427: 6712: //--- touching it is not adoptable mid-sequence.
P6428: 6713: //---
P6429: 6714: //--- The test is Ruling 8's in-play test, unchanged and threshold-free: BAR,
P6430: 6715: //--- SWING1, or SWING2 penetration of the candidate zone. Verified against the
P6431: 6716: //--- Task 35 journal - the 09:20 adoption of the operator's own XOB passes on
P6432: 6717: //--- BAR (close 1.16775 inside 1.16737-1.16778) and all three stale adoptions
P6433: 6718: //--- fail on all three conditions.
P6434: 6719: //---
P6435: 6720: //--- This does NOT re-adjudicate the existing zone. The `same` short-circuit
P6436: 6721: //--- returns true when the zone has not moved, so Task 35's boundary - in-play
P6437: 6722: //--- gates arming and is not re-checked afterwards - is preserved. What is
P6438: 6723: //--- gated here is a REPLACEMENT, which would otherwise bypass Ruling 7b's
P6439: 6724: //--- arming gate entirely.
P6440: 6725: //---
P6441: 6726: //--- The logic duplicates the S3 block's inline in-play test rather than
P6442: 6727: //--- sharing it, because extracting that block is a restructure and belongs to
P6443: 6728: //--- the state-machine rewrite. Logged as EA-49.
P6444: 6729: bool ZoneAdoptable(int barShift, double zHi, double zLo,
P6445: 6730:                    double stopRef, bool haveStop)
P6446: 6731:   {
P6447: 6732:    if(!(zHi > 0.0 && zLo > 0.0)) return false;
P6448: 6733:
P6449: 6734:    if(MathAbs(zHi - g_zoneHi) <= _Point * 0.5 &&
P6450: 6735:       MathAbs(zLo - g_zoneLo) <= _Point * 0.5)
P6451: 6736:       return true;
P6452: 6737:
P6453: 6738:    bool   ok  = false;
P6454: 6739:    string via = "none";
P6455: 6740:    double bHi = iHigh(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift);
P6456: 6741:    double bLo = iLow (_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift);
P6457: 6742:
P6458: 6743:    if(bHi >= zLo && bLo <= zHi) { ok = true; via = "BAR"; }
P6459: 6744:
P6460: 6745:    int    buf = (g_dir == DIR_LONG) ? FL_BUF_SWING_LOW : FL_BUF_SWING_HIGH;
P6461: 6746:    double sw1 = 0.0, sw2 = 0.0;
P6462: 6747:    int    sh1 = -1,  sh2 = -1;
P6463: 6748:
P6464: 6749:    if(FindNearestSwing(buf, barShift, sw1, sh1))
P6465: 6750:      {
P6466: 6751:        if(sw1 >= zLo && sw1 <= zHi)
P6467: 6752:          { ok = true; if(via == "none") via = "SWING1"; }
P6468: 6753:
P6469: 6754:        //--- [P-TRIM-S2POLL E3] loop-invariant hoist. Bars() cannot change within one
P6470: 6755:        //--- EvaluateClosedBar pass. Matches the existing t127_limit2 / t133_limit idiom.
P6471: 6756:        const int za_limit = barShift + Bars(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT);
P6472: 6757:        if(!haveStop)
P6473: 6758:         {
P6474: 6759:          for(int s = sh1 + 1; s <= sh1 + 500; s++)
P6475: 6760:            {
P6476: 6761:             double v2;
P6477: 6762:             if(!ReadFlow(buf, v2, s))          break;
P6478: 6763:             if(v2 == EMPTY_VALUE || v2 <= 0.0) continue;
P6479: 6764:             if(MathAbs(v2 - sw1) <= _Point)    continue;
P6480: 6765:             sw2 = v2; sh2 = s;
P6481: 6766:             break;
P6482: 6767:            }
P6483: 6768:
P6484: 6769:          if(sw2 > 0.0 && sw2 >= zLo && sw2 <= zHi)
P6485: 6770:            { ok = true; if(via == "none") via = "SWING2"; }
P6486: 6771:         }
P6487: 6772:       else
P6488: 6773:         {
P6489: 6774:          //--- [STEP 1] same SL-leg depth as ZoneInPlay: every confirmed
P6490: 6775:          //--- protective-side swing back to the stop reference, which ends the leg.
P6491: 6776:           double prev = sw1;
P6492: 6777:           for(int s = sh1 + 1; s <= za_limit; s++)
P6493: 6778:            {
P6494: 6779:             double v2;
P6495: 6780:             if(!ReadFlow(buf, v2, s))          break;
P6496: 6781:             if(v2 == EMPTY_VALUE || v2 <= 0.0) continue;
P6497: 6782:             if(MathAbs(v2 - prev) <= _Point)   continue;
P6498: 6783:             prev = v2;
P6499: 6784:             if(v2 >= zLo && v2 <= zHi)
P6500: 6785:               { ok = true; if(via == "none") via = "SWINGLEG"; }
P6501: 6786:             if((g_dir == DIR_LONG) ? (v2 <= stopRef) : (v2 >= stopRef)) break;
P6502: 6787:            }
P6503: 6788:         }
P6504: 6789:      }
P6505: 6790:
P6506: 6791:    if(InpDebugLog)
P6507: 6792:       PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA] ZONEADOPT bar=%s dir=%s adopt=%d via=%s newLo=%s newHi=%s "
P6508: 6793:                   "barLo=%s barHi=%s sw1=%s@%d sw2=%s@%d",
P6509: 6794:                   TimeToString(iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift), TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES),
P6510: 6795:                   DirName(g_dir), (int)ok, via,
P6511: 6796:                   DoubleToString(zLo, _Digits),
P6512: 6797:                   DoubleToString(zHi, _Digits),
P6513: 6798:                   DoubleToString(bLo, _Digits),
P6514: 6799:                   DoubleToString(bHi, _Digits),
P6515: 6800:                   (sw1 > 0.0 ? DoubleToString(sw1, _Digits) : "-"), sh1,
P6516: 6801:                   (sw2 > 0.0 ? DoubleToString(sw2, _Digits) : "-"), sh2);
P6517: 6802:    return ok;
P6518: 6803:   }
P6519: 6804:
P6520: 6805: //====================== [Task 52 / EA-59b] Leg-scoped touch scan ======
P6521: 6806: //--- Operator ruling EA-59b (Option B, wide): the opposite-direction touch may be
P6522: 6807: //--- ANY older bar within the current structural leg, not only a bar evaluated
P6523: 6808: //--- after the candidate armed. Admission is judged on the confirming bar with
P6524: 6809: //--- every condition simultaneously true; the touch is a leg-scoped fact, not a
P6525: 6810: //--- recency test, and retracement depth is not an admission criterion.
P6526: 6811: //---
P6527: 6812: //--- Measured cause: on 2026.08.20 the 09:35 candidate armed while evaluating the
P6528: 6813: //--- operator's own 09:30 entry bar - arming timing was correct - but the
P6529: 6814: //--- retracement into the zone had already completed before arming. The old S4
P6530: 6815: //--- test only ever examined the single bar it was evaluating, so no touch was
P6531: 6816: //--- ever seen and the sequence died at state=S4_ARMED. The EA was structurally
P6532: 6817: //--- one full touch-and-confirm cycle behind the operator, every time.
P6533: 6818: //---
P6534: 6819: //--- Bound is the STRUCTURAL leg boundary (buffer 30), read through ReadFlow so it
P6535: 6820: //--- lands in the settled slot. When the export reads 0.0 - unset, which is
P6536: 6821: //--- warmup only since UpstreamReady gates evaluation - the 500-slot cap is the
P6537: 6822: //--- sole bound. That cap matches FindNearestSwing and the 2-swing stop branch
P6538: 6823: //--- exactly; it is a safety limit, not a tunable threshold, so Part A section 7
P6539: 6824: //--- is not engaged.
P6540: 6825: //---
P6541: 6826: //--- The opposite-direction and zone-penetration tests below are
P6542: 6827: //--- character-for-character the tests the existing single-bar S4 block already
P6543: 6828: //--- applies. Nothing new is admitted per bar. The only change is how many bars
P6544: 6829: //--- are examined.
P6545: ```
P6546: ```mql5
P6547: 2191: void ShadowRetestBook(const int barShift)
P6548: 2192:   {
P6549: 2193:    if(!InpDebugLog || !SHADOW_RETESTBOOK) return;
P6550: 2194:    double o = iOpen (_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift);
P6551: 2195:    double h = iHigh (_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift);
P6552: 2196:    double l = iLow  (_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift);
P6553: 2197:    double c = iClose(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift);
P6554: 2198:    if(h <= 0.0 || l <= 0.0) return;
P6555: 2199:    double cNext = (barShift >= 1) ? iOpen(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift - 1) : 0.0;
P6556: 2200:    if(cNext <= 0.0) cNext = c;
P6557: 2201:    double bodyHi = MathMax(o, cNext);
P6558: 2202:    double bodyLo = MathMin(o, cNext);
P6559: 2203:    double P   = _Point;
P6560: 2204:    double EPS = P * 0.001;
P6561: 2205:    string hits = "";
P6562: 2206:    int    nHits = 0;
P6563: 2207:    for(int k = 0; k < POI_NLINES; k++)
P6564: 2208:      {
P6565: 2209:       double L;
P6566: 2210:       if(!ReadBuf1(g_hPoi, k, L, barShift)) continue;
P6567: 2211:       if(L == EMPTY_VALUE || L <= 0.0)      continue;
P6568: 2212:       bool longHit  = (l <= L - P + EPS && bodyLo >= L - EPS);
P6569: 2213:       bool shortHit = (h >= L + P - EPS && bodyHi <= L + EPS);
P6570: 2214:       if(!longHit && !shortHit) continue;
P6571: 2215:       if(nHits > 0) hits += " ";
P6572: 2216:       hits += g_lineCode[k] + ":r" + IntegerToString(g_authorityRank[k]) +
P6573: 2217:               ":d" + (longHit ? "L" : "S");
P6574: 2218:       nHits++;
P6575: 2219:      }

P6603: 2247: void ShadowRetestNearMiss(const int barShift)
P6604: 2248:    {

P6614: 2258:     double distB = 0.0;

P6616: 2260:     bool haveB = false;

P6630: 2274:        if(L < l && (!haveB || (l - L) < distB))

P6632: 2276:       }

P6635: 2279:                 TimeToString(iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift),
P6636: 2280:                              TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES),
P6637: 2281:                 inside,
P6638: 2282:                 codeA, (haveA ? DoubleToString(distA / P, 1) : "-"),
P6639: 2283:                 codeB, (haveB ? DoubleToString(distB / P, 1) : "-"));
P6640: 2284:    }
P6641: 2285: //--- CONFIRMPOLL: the spec section 3.6 confirmation-candle terms, per bar, as data.
P6642: 2286: //--- term A oppCandle: the PRIOR candle closed AGAINST the direction (the retracement/
P6643: 2287: //---   opposing candle). term B bodyDir: the CURRENT candle closes IN the direction with
P6644: 2288: //---   any nonzero body (only a true doji rejected - the section 3.6 rule). term C
P6645: 2289: //---   touchAttr: the PRIOR candle touched the anchor line (wick through the line value).
P6646: 2290: //--- confirm = A && B && C. This is the calibration surface for build 2's
P6647: 2291: //--- IsConfirmationCandle; nothing here gates anything.
P6648: 2292: void ShadowConfirmPoll(const int barShift, const int anchorLine, const ENUM_SRJ_DIR dir)
P6649: 2293:   {
P6650: 2294:    if(!InpDebugLog || !SHADOW_CONFIRMPOLL) return;
P6651: 2295:    if(anchorLine < 0 || dir == DIR_NONE)   return;
P6652: 2296:    double o1 = iOpen (_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift + 1);
P6653: 2297:    double c1 = iClose(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift + 1);
P6654: 2298:    double h1 = iHigh (_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift + 1);
P6655: 2299:    double l1 = iLow  (_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift + 1);
P6656: 2300:    double o0 = iOpen (_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift);
P6657: 2301:    double c0 = iClose(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift);
P6658: 2302:    if(o1 <= 0.0 || c1 <= 0.0 || o0 <= 0.0 || c0 <= 0.0) return;
P6659: 2303:    double L;
P6660: 2304:    if(!ReadBuf1(g_hPoi, anchorLine, L, barShift)) return;
P6661: 2305:    if(L == EMPTY_VALUE || L <= 0.0)               return;
P6662: 2306:    bool oppCandle = (dir == DIR_LONG)  ? (c1 < o1) : (c1 > o1);
P6663: 2307:    double body    = MathAbs(c0 - o0);
P6664: 2308:    bool   isDoji  = (body < _Point * 0.0001);
P6665: 2309:    bool   bodyDir = (dir == DIR_LONG)  ? (c0 > o0) : (c0 < o0);
P6666: 2310:    bool   touch   = (h1 >= L - _Point && l1 <= L + _Point);
P6667: 2311:    bool   confirm = (oppCandle && bodyDir && !isDoji && touch);
P6668: 2312:    PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA] CONFIRMPOLL bar=%s anchor=%s dir=%s oppCandle=%d bodyDir=%d "
P6669: 2313:                "body=%dpts doji=%d touchAttr=%d confirm=%d shadow=true",
P6670: 2314:                TimeToString(iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift),
P6671: 2315:                             TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES),
P6672: 2316:                g_lineCode[anchorLine], DirName(dir),
P6673: 2317:                (int)oppCandle, (int)bodyDir, (int)MathRound(body / _Point),
P6674: 2318:                (int)isDoji, (int)touch, (int)confirm);
P6675: 2319:   }
P6676: 2320:
P6677: 2321: //--- [P-CONFIRM-GATE E1 2026-09-10] build 2: the confirmation predicate is now a
P6678: 2322: //--- real function and the LIVE GATE (it replaces the poll-only role of
P6679: 2323: //--- ShadowConfirmPoll above, which keeps printing the same terms as the gate's
P6680: 2324: //--- trace - shadow=true naming for continuity). Terms (packet section 1; the
P6681: 2325: //--- operator's ruled retracement term A2 included):
P6682: 2326: //---   A  the prior candle (barShift+1) closed AGAINST dir (the retracement/
P6683: 2327: //---      opposing candle);
P6684: 2328: //---   A2 RULED: the prior candle's CLOSE stays on the SETUP SIDE of the anchor
P6685: 2329: //---      line (a wick through is the retracement; a CLOSE through is a line
P6686: 2330: //---      break - no confirmation after a break; LONG: close >= line;
P6687: 2331: //---      SHORT: close <= line; applies to VWAP and POC alike);
P6688: 2332: //---   B  the current candle (barShift) closes IN dir with any nonzero body

P6690: 2334: //---   C  the prior candle's range touched the anchor line (the CONFIRMPOLL
P6691: 2335: //---      touchAttr test with its +/- 1 point guard).
P6692: 2336: //--- failTerm names the FIRST failed term ("" = all terms passed).

P6695: 2076: bool DetectPoiRetest(int barShift, PoiRetestResult &r)
P6696: 2077:   {

P6704: 2085:    //--- test is evaluated at the NEXT candle's OPEN, not the retest candle's
P6705: 2086:    //--- close (Part A spec section 4: evaluate at the next candle's open).

P6723: 2104:     int n1e_nW = 0, n1e_nB = 0;

P6728: 2109:        //--- [P-SLDEF-1 E14] N1 counters: exact-equality encounters, counted
P6729: 2110:        //--- without branching (outcome untouched). Grounding: LONG survives
P6730: 2111:        //--- iff the wick pierces (l <= L-P+EPS) AND the body holds
P6731: 2112:        //--- (bodyLo >= L-EPS); equality on either term passes. SHORT mirror.
P6732: 2113:         if(l == L || h == L) { g_n1_poiEqWick++; n1e_nW++; }
P6733: 2114:         if(bodyLo == L || bodyHi == L) { g_n1_poiEqBody++; n1e_nB++; }
P6734: 2115:        if(l <= L - P + EPS && bodyLo >= L - EPS)

P6736: 2117:       if(h >= L + P - EPS && bodyHi <= L + EPS)

P6738: 2119:      }

P6741: 2122:     if(bestLongLine >= 0 && (bestShortLine < 0 || bestLongRank <= bestShortRank))
